19595:       // High roughness = severe step-downs → crowding / rotation. Moderate = uneven incisal.
19596:       // hasCrowding is used to suppress diastema detection (crowding shadows mimic gaps in 2D).
19597:       const cols=[]; for(let x=minX;x<=maxX;x++){ if(colMaxY[x]>=0) cols.push(colMaxY[x]); }
19598:       let hasCrowdingFinding=false;

19602:         else if(rough/bh>0.04) F.push({en:'uneven incisal edges (mild irregularity or wear)',ja:'切縁の不揃い・軽度摩耗'});

19605:       //   (a) brackets present (bracket slots and wire gaps mimic open spaces), OR
19606:       //   (b) crowding detected — in 2D photography, overlapping / lingually displaced teeth
19607:       //       cast deep shadows that look like gaps. Crowding and diastema are clinically
19608:       //       MUTUALLY EXCLUSIVE; when crowding is present every dark interproximal region
19609:       //       must be treated as a depth shadow, never as an open interdental space.
19610:       if(!hasBrackets && !hasCrowdingFinding){

19612:         for(let x=minX;x<=maxX;x++){ let t=false; for(let y=y0;y<=y1;y++){ if(mask[y*W+x]>0.5){t=true;break;} } if(!t){run++;if(run>maxRun)maxRun=run;}else run=0; }
19613:         if(maxRun/bw>0.06) F.push({en:'spacing / diastema between teeth',ja:'歯間離開（すきっ歯）'});
19614:       }

19640:   async function ensureProblemList(src){
19641:     if(!src || !src.dataUrl) return [];
19642:     if(Array.isArray(src.problemList)) return src.problemList;
19643:     src.problemList = await buildProblemList(src.dataUrl);
19644:     if(src.problemList && src.problemList.__structured && !src.structuredFindings){
19645:       src.structuredFindings = src.problemList.__structured;
19646:     }
19647:     return src.problemList;
19648:   }
19649:   window.caseflowEnsureProblemList = ensureProblemList;

19651:   // Pixel-level Bio-Blend overlay for intraoral contraster shots. BIDIRECTIONAL:
19652:   //   bb > 0 => naturalization microstructure: マメロン / 周波条 / ハロー効果
19653:   //   bb < 0 => SMOOTH: minimise surface texture / anatomy, emphasise a smooth even surface
19654:   // ox = 2d context of the source image (used to build the enamel mask).
19655:   // ctx = output 2d context to draw effects onto.
19656:   // bb = signed bio-blend strength, -1..+1.
19657:   function applyIntraoralBioBlend(ctx, ox, w, h, bb){
19658:     if(Math.abs(bb) <= 0.01) return;
19659:     const smooth = bb < 0;
19660:     const amt = Math.abs(bb);   // 0..1
19661:     const em = enamelMaskCanvas(ox, w, h);
19662:     if(!em) return;
19663:     const emx = em.getContext('2d', {willReadFrequently: true});
19664:     const emD = emx.getImageData(0, 0, w, h).data;
19665:     let id; try{ id = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, w, h); } catch(e){ return; }
19666:     const d = id.data;
19667:     // For the SMOOTH side we blend each enamel pixel toward a blurred copy of itself, which
19668:     // evens out perikymata / mammellons / micro-texture and emphasises a smooth surface.
19669:     let blurD=null;
19670:     if(smooth){
19671:       try{
19672:         const bc=document.createElement('canvas'); bc.width=w; bc.height=h;
19673:         const bx=bc.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});
19674:         bx.filter='blur('+Math.max(1,Math.round(Math.min(w,h)*0.004*(0.5+amt)))+'px)';
19675:         bx.drawImage(ctx.canvas,0,0); bx.filter='none';
19676:         blurD=bx.getImageData(0,0,w,h).data;
19677:       }catch(e){ blurD=null; }
19678:     }
19679:     // find incisal (top) and cervical (bottom) extent of the enamel band
19680:     let minY = h, maxY = 0;
19681:     for(let i = 0; i < emD.length; i += 4){
19682:       if(emD[i] > 64){
19683:         const py = ((i >> 2) / w) | 0;
19684:         if(py < minY) minY = py;
19685:         if(py > maxY) maxY = py;
19686:       }
19687:     }
19688:     if(minY >= maxY) return;
19689:     const enamelH = maxY - minY;
19690:     for(let y = minY; y <= maxY; y++){
19691:       const relY = (y - minY) / enamelH; // 0 = incisal edge, 1 = gingival
19692:       for(let x = 0; x < w; x++){
19693:         const qi = y * w + x;
19694:         const m = emD[qi << 2] / 255; // enamel mask weight at this pixel
19695:         if(m < 0.04) continue;
19696:         const p = qi << 2;
19697:         let r = d[p], g = d[p+1], b = d[p+2];
19698:         if(!smooth){
19699:           // ===== Naturalization (right side) =====
19700:           // ハロー効果 — incisal translucency: blue-grey shift in top 12% of enamel band
19701:           if(relY < 0.12){
19702:             const t = Math.pow(1 - relY / 0.12, 1.6);
19703:             const halo = t * amt * 0.20 * m;
19704:             const lum = (r + g + b) / 3;
19705:             r = clamp(r + (lum * 0.80 - r) * halo, 0, 255);
19706:             g = clamp(g + (lum * 0.90 - g) * halo, 0, 255);
19707:             b = clamp(b + (Math.min(255, lum * 1.06 + 14) - b) * halo, 0, 255);
19708:           }
19709:           // 周波条 — fine horizontal perikymata ridges every 4 px, concentrated near incisal
19710:           const periW = Math.max(0, 1 - relY * 2.2) * amt * 0.030 * m;
19711:           if(periW > 0.001){
19712:             const periMod = Math.sin((y % 4) * Math.PI * 0.5) * periW;
19713:             r = clamp(r + periMod * 65, 0, 255);
19714:             g = clamp(g + periMod * 65, 0, 255);
19715:             b = clamp(b + periMod * 65, 0, 255);
19716:           }
19717:           // マメロン — 3 developmental lobe brightness variations at incisal third
19718:           if(relY < 0.28){
19719:             const mamZ = Math.pow(1 - relY / 0.28, 1.3);
19720:             const lobeAmp = Math.cos(x / w * Math.PI * 6) * 0.5 + 0.5;
19721:             const mamS = lobeAmp * mamZ * amt * 0.050 * m;
19722:             const lum = (r + g + b) / 3;
19723:             r = clamp(r + (lum + 5 - r) * mamS, 0, 255);
19724:             g = clamp(g + (lum + 5 - g) * mamS, 0, 255);
19725:             b = clamp(b + (lum + 9 - b) * mamS, 0, 255);
19726:           }
19727:         } else {
19728:           // ===== Smooth (left side) — minimise surface texture / anatomy =====
19729:           if(blurD){
19730:             const k = amt * 0.92 * m;   // blend toward the blurred copy: evens out micro-texture
19731:             r += (blurD[p]   - r) * k;
19732:             g += (blurD[p+1] - g) * k;
19733:             b += (blurD[p+2] - b) * k;
19734:           }
19735:         }
19736:         d[p] = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, r)) | 0; d[p+1] = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, g)) | 0; d[p+2] = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, b)) | 0;
19737:       }
19738:     }
19739:     ctx.putImageData(id, 0, 0);
19740:   }
19741: 
19742:   // ── Sim Studio landmark-aware multi-zone ROI + distance-based soft matte ──────
19743:   // Maps treatment concept + active chief complaint cards → not just Support/Context
19744:   // *extent* but also the directional *shape* of the ROI (horizontal widening toward
19745:   // the commissures, vertical extension toward the gingiva or the incisal edge).
19746:   // This is what makes each card change the ROI's FORM, not only a magnitude value.
19747:   function cfSimROIConfig(concept, targets){
19748:     // radial extent multipliers — context defines the outermost falloff radius
19749:     let support=1.55, context=2.15;
19750:     // directional shape bias (neutral = 1.0 / 0.0)
19751:     let biasX=1.0;     // horizontal widening toward the mouth corners
19752:     let biasUp=0.0;    // upward (gingival) extension, as a fraction of ry
19753:     let biasDown=0.0;  // downward (incisal / lower-lip) extension, fraction of ry
19754:     // ---- treatment concept shapes the base ROI ----
19755:     if(concept==='prosthetic'){ support=1.75; context=2.35; biasUp=0.10; }        // target tooth unit + gingival emergence
19756:     else if(concept==='hybrid'){ support=2.00; context=2.85; biasX=1.14; biasUp=0.12; biasDown=0.10; } // widest multi-zone
19757:     else { support=1.60; context=2.20; biasX=1.18; biasDown=0.06; }               // ortho: arch / smile-line → widen toward corners
19758:     const t=targets||{};
19759:     // ---- chief-complaint cards reshape the ROI (form, not just value) ----
19760:     if(t.shade){   support=Math.max(support,1.45); }                              // tightest: teeth-centred, thin gingiva/lip support
19761:     if(t.space){   support=Math.max(support,1.85); context=Math.max(context,2.55); biasX=Math.max(biasX,1.16); } // interproximal units, wider
19762:     if(t.scallop){ support=Math.max(support,1.90); context=Math.max(context,2.55); biasUp=Math.max(biasUp,0.24); }  // gingiva → extend upward
19763:     if(t.incisal){ support=Math.max(support,1.70); context=Math.max(context,2.40); biasDown=Math.max(biasDown,0.24); } // incisal + lower-lip → downward
19764:     if(t.missing){ support=Math.max(support,2.00); context=Math.max(context,2.80); biasX=Math.max(biasX,1.16); biasUp=Math.max(biasUp,0.12); } // edentulous + adjacent + gingiva
19765:     if(t.harmony){ support=Math.max(support,2.25); context=Math.max(context,3.05); biasX=Math.max(biasX,1.22); biasDown=Math.max(biasDown,0.10); } // widest perioral context
19766:     return {core:1.0, support:support, context:context, biasX:biasX, biasUp:biasUp, biasDown:biasDown};
19767:   }
19768: 
19769:   // Continuous distance-based soft matte (NO banding).  A multi-stop radial gradient
19770:   // encodes the Core→Support→Context edit contribution as a SMOOTH falloff from the
19771:   // ROI centre (the radial coordinate is the distance, so the decay is continuous —
19772:   // unlike stacked discrete-alpha ellipses which leave visible bands).  An anisotropic
19773:   // transform + directional bias give the ROI its per-card SHAPE; an optional feathered
19774:   // landmark polygon clips the matte to the real mouth contour (facial) so the result
19775:   // follows anatomy instead of a bare symmetric ellipse.
19776:   //   Core      → alpha ≈ 1.00  dental crowns / incisal / spaces / restoration
19777:   //   Inner Sup → alpha ≈ 0.70  papilla / gingival margin / inner lip
19778:   //   Outer Sup → alpha ≈ 0.40  gingiva / lip surface / commissure
19779:   //   Context   → alpha ≈ 0.20  perioral skin / sub-nasal / chin shadow → 0
19780:   function cfBuildSoftMatte(w, h, cx, cy, rx, ry, scales, featherPx, shapePoly){
19781:     const off=document.createElement('canvas'); off.width=w; off.height=h;
19782:     const ox=off.getContext('2d');
19783:     const biasX=scales.biasX||1.0;
19784:     const upPx=(scales.biasUp||0)*ry;     // gingival extension (pixels)
19785:     const downPx=(scales.biasDown||0)*ry; // incisal extension (pixels)
19786:     // Outermost extent = the context ring → the gradient's unit radius (=1).
19787:     const Rx=Math.max(2, rx*scales.context*biasX);
19788:     const Ry=Math.max(2, ry*scales.context);
19789:     // Vertical centre shift toward the card's anatomical target (gingiva ↑ / incisal ↓).

19700:           // ハロー効果 — incisal translucency: blue-grey shift in top 12% of enamel band
19701:           if(relY < 0.12){
19702:             const t = Math.pow(1 - relY / 0.12, 1.6);
19703:             const halo = t * amt * 0.20 * m;
19704:             const lum = (r + g + b) / 3;
19705:             r = clamp(r + (lum * 0.80 - r) * halo, 0, 255);
19706:             g = clamp(g + (lum * 0.90 - g) * halo, 0, 255);
19707:             b = clamp(b + (Math.min(255, lum * 1.06 + 14) - b) * halo, 0, 255);
19708:           }
19709:           // 周波条 — fine horizontal perikymata ridges every 4 px, concentrated near incisal
19710:           const periW = Math.max(0, 1 - relY * 2.2) * amt * 0.030 * m;
19711:           if(periW > 0.001){
19712:             const periMod = Math.sin((y % 4) * Math.PI * 0.5) * periW;
19713:             r = clamp(r + periMod * 65, 0, 255);
19714:             g = clamp(g + periMod * 65, 0, 255);
19715:             b = clamp(b + periMod * 65, 0, 255);
19716:           }
19717:           // マメロン — 3 developmental lobe brightness variations at incisal third
19718:           if(relY < 0.28){
19719:             const mamZ = Math.pow(1 - relY / 0.28, 1.3);
19720:             const lobeAmp = Math.cos(x / w * Math.PI * 6) * 0.5 + 0.5;
19721:             const mamS = lobeAmp * mamZ * amt * 0.050 * m;
19722:             const lum = (r + g + b) / 3;
19723:             r = clamp(r + (lum + 5 - r) * mamS, 0, 255);
19724:             g = clamp(g + (lum + 5 - g) * mamS, 0, 255);
19725:             b = clamp(b + (lum + 9 - b) * mamS, 0, 255);
19726:           }
19727:         } else {
19728:           // ===== Smooth (left side) — minimise surface texture / anatomy =====
19729:           if(blurD){
19730:             const k = amt * 0.92 * m;   // blend toward the blurred copy: evens out micro-texture
19731:             r += (blurD[p]   - r) * k;
19732:             g += (blurD[p+1] - g) * k;
19733:             b += (blurD[p+2] - b) * k;
19734:           }
19735:         }
19736:         d[p] = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, r)) | 0; d[p+1] = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, g)) | 0; d[p+2] = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, b)) | 0;
19737:       }
19738:     }
19739:     ctx.putImageData(id, 0, 0);
19740:   }
19741: 
19742:   // ── Sim Studio landmark-aware multi-zone ROI + distance-based soft matte ──────
19743:   // Maps treatment concept + active chief complaint cards → not just Support/Context
19744:   // *extent* but also the directional *shape* of the ROI (horizontal widening toward
19745:   // the commissures, vertical extension toward the gingiva or the incisal edge).
19746:   // This is what makes each card change the ROI's FORM, not only a magnitude value.
19747:   function cfSimROIConfig(concept, targets){
19748:     // radial extent multipliers — context defines the outermost falloff radius
19749:     let support=1.55, context=2.15;
19750:     // directional shape bias (neutral = 1.0 / 0.0)
19751:     let biasX=1.0;     // horizontal widening toward the mouth corners
19752:     let biasUp=0.0;    // upward (gingival) extension, as a fraction of ry
19753:     let biasDown=0.0;  // downward (incisal / lower-lip) extension, fraction of ry
19754:     // ---- treatment concept shapes the base ROI ----
19755:     if(concept==='prosthetic'){ support=1.75; context=2.35; biasUp=0.10; }        // target tooth unit + gingival emergence
19756:     else if(concept==='hybrid'){ support=2.00; context=2.85; biasX=1.14; biasUp=0.12; biasDown=0.10; } // widest multi-zone
19757:     else { support=1.60; context=2.20; biasX=1.18; biasDown=0.06; }               // ortho: arch / smile-line → widen toward corners
19758:     const t=targets||{};
19759:     // ---- chief-complaint cards reshape the ROI (form, not just value) ----
19760:     if(t.shade){   support=Math.max(support,1.45); }                              // tightest: teeth-centred, thin gingiva/lip support
19761:     if(t.space){   support=Math.max(support,1.85); context=Math.max(context,2.55); biasX=Math.max(biasX,1.16); } // interproximal units, wider
19762:     if(t.scallop){ support=Math.max(support,1.90); context=Math.max(context,2.55); biasUp=Math.max(biasUp,0.24); }  // gingiva → extend upward
19763:     if(t.incisal){ support=Math.max(support,1.70); context=Math.max(context,2.40); biasDown=Math.max(biasDown,0.24); } // incisal + lower-lip → downward
19764:     if(t.missing){ support=Math.max(support,2.00); context=Math.max(context,2.80); biasX=Math.max(biasX,1.16); biasUp=Math.max(biasUp,0.12); } // edentulous + adjacent + gingiva
19765:     if(t.harmony){ support=Math.max(support,2.25); context=Math.max(context,3.05); biasX=Math.max(biasX,1.22); biasDown=Math.max(biasDown,0.10); } // widest perioral context
19766:     return {core:1.0, support:support, context:context, biasX:biasX, biasUp:biasUp, biasDown:biasDown};
19767:   }
19768: 
19769:   // Continuous distance-based soft matte (NO banding).  A multi-stop radial gradient
19770:   // encodes the Core→Support→Context edit contribution as a SMOOTH falloff from the
19771:   // ROI centre (the radial coordinate is the distance, so the decay is continuous —
19772:   // unlike stacked discrete-alpha ellipses which leave visible bands).  An anisotropic
19773:   // transform + directional bias give the ROI its per-card SHAPE; an optional feathered
19774:   // landmark polygon clips the matte to the real mouth contour (facial) so the result
19775:   // follows anatomy instead of a bare symmetric ellipse.
19776:   //   Core      → alpha ≈ 1.00  dental crowns / incisal / spaces / restoration
19777:   //   Inner Sup → alpha ≈ 0.70  papilla / gingival margin / inner lip
19778:   //   Outer Sup → alpha ≈ 0.40  gingiva / lip surface / commissure
19779:   //   Context   → alpha ≈ 0.20  perioral skin / sub-nasal / chin shadow → 0
19780:   function cfBuildSoftMatte(w, h, cx, cy, rx, ry, scales, featherPx, shapePoly){
19781:     const off=document.createElement('canvas'); off.width=w; off.height=h;
19782:     const ox=off.getContext('2d');
19783:     const biasX=scales.biasX||1.0;
19784:     const upPx=(scales.biasUp||0)*ry;     // gingival extension (pixels)
19785:     const downPx=(scales.biasDown||0)*ry; // incisal extension (pixels)
19786:     // Outermost extent = the context ring → the gradient's unit radius (=1).
19787:     const Rx=Math.max(2, rx*scales.context*biasX);
19788:     const Ry=Math.max(2, ry*scales.context);
19789:     // Vertical centre shift toward the card's anatomical target (gingiva ↑ / incisal ↓).
===== caseflowLocalAnalyze: [6208, 8681, 9399, 16242, 16243, 16286, 16298, 20637, 20640] =====
06163:   };

06351:   async function callAISimulation(){
06352:     const source = state.simMicro || state.simMacro || state.photos.find(p=>p.category==='focus') || state.photos[0];
06353:     if(!source){ toast('先に Macro / Facial と Micro / Focus を設定してください'); return false; }
06354:     toast('AI Simulation backend に接続中…');
06355:     try{
06356:       const res = await fetch('/api/ai_simulation',{
06357:         method:'POST',
08636:     //     upload-time popup acts as the manual safety net for the remaining edge cases).
08637:     const intraoralDark = (darkBorderRatio>0.34 || bgBright<92) && mouthRatio>0.18;
08638:     // (B) Retracted intraoral shot (cheek retractors, NO black contraster): the frame is
08639:     //     filled with oral content (teeth + gingiva), there is essentially no facial skin
08640:     //     spread, and it is a wide (landscape) close-up rather than a tall portrait. This
08641:     //     catches ordinary retracted intraoral photos that have a normal (non-black) bg.
08642:     const faceArea = faceBox ? faceBox.w*faceBox.h : 0;
08643:     const noFace = faceArea < 0.22;
08644:     const landscape = img.naturalWidth >= img.naturalHeight*0.95;
08645:     const toothFrac = whiteInside/(W*H);
08646:     const darkFrac  = darkCount/(W*H);
08647:     const retracted = mouthRatio>0.36 && noFace && landscape && toothFrac>0.015 && darkFrac>0.02;
08648:     const intraoral = intraoralDark || retracted;
08649:     if(intraoral && !forcedCategory) category='focus';   // any intraoral close-up is Focus
08650:     const mouth = lipBox || (category==='facial' ? {x:contentBox.x+contentBox.w*.30,y:contentBox.y+contentBox.h*.55,w:contentBox.w*.40,h:contentBox.h*.18} : {x:contentBox.x+contentBox.w*.08,y:contentBox.y+contentBox.h*.28,w:contentBox.w*.84,h:contentBox.h*.44});
08651:     const teethFinal = teethBox || {x:mouth.x+mouth.w*.18,y:mouth.y+mouth.h*.33,w:mouth.w*.64,h:mouth.h*.32};
08652:     return {
08653:       category,
08654:       intraoral,
08655:       intraoralDark,
08656:       confidence: category==='facial' ? (faceBox?.count?0.82:0.66) : ((redCount+whiteInside+darkCount)>80?0.82:0.62),
08657:       landmarks:{content:contentBox,face:faceBox||contentBox,mouth,teeth:teethFinal,midline:teethFinal.x+teethFinal.w*.5,incisal:teethFinal.y+teethFinal.h*.70,gingival:teethFinal.y+teethFinal.h*.18}
08658:     };
08659:   }
08660:   async function localAnalyze(dataUrl,forcedCategory){
08661:     const img=await loadImg(dataUrl);
08662:     let browserFace=null;
08663:     // Experimental browser-native detector. It gives only face boxes, not dental landmarks.
08664:     try{
08665:       if('FaceDetector' in window && !forcedCategory){
08666:         const fd=new FaceDetector({fastMode:true,maxDetectedFaces:1});
08667:         const faces=await fd.detect(img);
08668:         if(faces && faces[0]) browserFace=faces[0].boundingBox;
08669:       }
08670:     }catch(e){}
08671:     const result=analyzePixels(img,forcedCategory);
08672:     if(browserFace && !result.intraoral){
08673:       result.category='facial';
08674:       result.landmarks.face={x:browserFace.x/img.naturalWidth,y:browserFace.y/img.naturalHeight,w:browserFace.width/img.naturalWidth,h:browserFace.height/img.naturalHeight};
08675:       const f=result.landmarks.face;
08676:       result.landmarks.mouth={x:f.x+f.w*.28,y:f.y+f.h*.58,w:f.w*.44,h:f.h*.18};
08677:       result.confidence=.86;
08678:     }
08679:     return result;
08680:   }
08681:   window.caseflowLocalAnalyze = localAnalyze;
08682: 
08683:   // Upload-time confirmation popup: lets the clinician confirm/override how a Focus photo
08684:   // was taken (black contraster / ordinary retracted intraoral / not an intraoral close-up),
08685:   // since reflections or unusual backgrounds can fool the automatic detector. Resolves to
08686:   // { intraoral, intraoralDark, manual }. `auto` = { intraoral, intraoralDark } | null.
08687:   window.cfAskContraster = function(auto){
08688:     return new Promise(resolve=>{
08689:       const autoCat = (auto && auto.category) || '';
08690:       const autoKey = (auto && auto.intraoral)
08691:         ? (auto.intraoralDark ? 'dark' : 'retracted')
08692:         : (autoCat==='facial' ? 'facial' : 'focuslips');
08693:       // shot type => { intraoral, intraoralDark, lips }
08694:       const MAP = {
08695:         facial:    {intraoral:false, intraoralDark:false, lips:true },
08696:         focuslips: {intraoral:false, intraoralDark:false, lips:true },
08697:         retracted: {intraoral:true,  intraoralDark:false, lips:false},
08698:         dark:      {intraoral:true,  intraoralDark:true,  lips:false}
08699:       };
08700:       const labelOf = k => k==='dark' ? '黒コントラスター（リップなし）'
08701:         : k==='retracted' ? 'リトラクター（リップなし）'
08702:         : k==='facial' ? '顔貌・スマイル（リップあり）'
08703:         : '口腔内接写・リップあり';
08704:       if(!document.getElementById('cfCtStyle')){
08705:         const st=document.createElement('style'); st.id='cfCtStyle';
08706:         st.textContent='.cfCtOverlay{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:99999;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:rgba(16,12,30,.55);backdrop-filter:blur(3px);padding:22px}'
08707:           +'.cfCtCard{width:100%;max-width:360px;background:var(--cfUiPanelStrong,#fff);color:var(--cfUiText,#201b35);border-radius:18px;padding:18px 16px 14px;box-shadow:0 22px 60px rgba(16,12,30,.4)}'
08708:           +'.cfCtTitle{font-size:15px;font-weight:950;margin:0 2px 4px}'
08709:           +'.cfCtSub{font-size:11px;line-height:1.5;color:var(--cfUiSub,rgba(32,27,53,.6));margin:0 2px 14px}'
08710:           +'.cfCtBtn{display:block;width:100%;text-align:left;border:1.5px solid var(--cfUiBorder,rgba(32,27,53,.16));background:var(--cfUiPanel,rgba(255,255,255,.6));color:inherit;border-radius:13px;padding:11px 13px;margin-bottom:9px;font:inherit;font-size:13px;font-weight:850;cursor:pointer;transition:border-color .15s,background .15s}'
08711:           +'.cfCtBtn small{display:block;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:600;color:var(--cfUiSub,rgba(32,27,53,.55));margin-top:3px}'
08712:           +'.cfCtBtn:hover{border-color:var(--c1,#6a4df4)}'
08713:           +'.cfCtBtn.cfCtSel{border-color:var(--c1,#6a4df4);background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--c1,#6a4df4) 12%,transparent)}'
08714:           +'.cfCtBtn.cfCtSel::after{content:" ✓ 自動判定";font-size:10px;font-weight:900;color:var(--c1,#6a4df4)}'
08715:           +'.cfCtSkip{display:block;width:100%;text-align:center;background:none;border:none;color:var(--cfUiSub,rgba(32,27,53,.55));font:inherit;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;padding:8px 4px 2px;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline}';
08716:         document.head.appendChild(st);
08717:       }
08718:       const ov=document.createElement('div'); ov.className='cfCtOverlay';
08719:       ov.innerHTML='<div class="cfCtCard">'
08720:         +'<div class="cfCtTitle">この写真の撮影タイプは？</div>'
08721:         +'<div class="cfCtSub">リップが写っているか／コントラスターの有無で生成処理が変わります（リップなしの接写ではリップを生成しません）。'+(auto?('自動判定: <b>'+labelOf(autoKey)+'</b>'):'')+'</div>'
08722:         +'<button class="cfCtBtn" data-v="facial">顔貌・スマイル（リップあり）<small>顔全体／口元のスマイル写真。歯のみ補正し、顔・リップはそのまま</small></button>'
08723:         +'<button class="cfCtBtn" data-v="focuslips">口腔内接写・リップあり<small>リトラクターなしの接写でリップが写っている。リップはそのまま、歯のみ補正</small></button>'
08724:         +'<button class="cfCtBtn" data-v="retracted">リトラクター（リップなし）<small>頬粘膜を開けた接写・黒背景なし。リップは生成しない／歯肉・背景はそのまま</small></button>'
08725:         +'<button class="cfCtBtn" data-v="dark">黒コントラスター（リップなし）<small>黒背景・single arch の接写。背景を黒のまま、エナメルのみ補正</small></button>'

08727:         +'</div>';

08729:       const done=(v)=>{
08730:         ov.remove();
08731:         if(v==='auto'){
08732:           const ai=!!(auto&&auto.intraoral);
08733:           resolve({intraoral:ai, intraoralDark:!!(auto&&auto.intraoralDark), lips:!ai, manual:false});
08734:           return;
08735:         }
08736:         const m=MAP[v]||MAP.focuslips;
08737:         resolve({ intraoral:m.intraoral, intraoralDark:m.intraoralDark, lips:m.lips, manual:true });
08738:       };
08739:       ov.querySelectorAll('[data-v]').forEach(b=>{ if(b.dataset.v===autoKey) b.classList.add('cfCtSel'); b.addEventListener('click',()=>done(b.dataset.v)); });
08740:       ov.addEventListener('click',e=>{ if(e.target===ov) done('auto'); });
08741:     });
08742:   };

08744:   function overlayMarkup(p){
08745:     const lm=p.landmarks;if(!lm)return '';
08746:     const box=(b,cls)=>b?`<div class="box ${cls}" style="left:${b.x*100}%;top:${b.y*100}%;width:${b.w*100}%;height:${b.h*100}%"></div>`:'';
08747:     const lineV=lm.midline?`<div class="line v" style="left:${lm.midline*100}%"></div>`:'';
08748:     const lineH=lm.incisal?`<div class="line h" style="top:${lm.incisal*100}%"></div>`:'';
08749:     return `<div class="landmarkOverlay">${box(lm.mouth,'mouth')}${box(lm.teeth,'teeth')}${lineV}${lineH}</div>`;
08750:   }
08751:   function toolButtons(i,p){
08752:     return `<div class="tileActions"><button class="miniBtn" onclick="event.stopPropagation();runLocalDetect(${i})">Detect</button><button class="miniBtn subtle" onclick="event.stopPropagation();openCropModal(${i})">Crop</button><button class="miniBtn subtle" onclick="event.stopPropagation();removeFacialBg(${i})">BG</button></div>`;
08753:   }
08754:   window.renderUpload=function(){

08762:         html += `<div class="tile categoryPriority"><img src="${p.dataUrl}">${overlayMarkup(p)}<span class="badge">${i+1}</span>${intraoralBadge}<div class="landmarkHint"><span>${p.intraoral?'Focus · 口腔内接写 (contraster)':(p.landmarks?'local landmarks':'facial / focus')}</span></div><button class="x" onclick="removePhoto(${i})">×</button><div class="ord"><button onclick="movePhoto(${i},-1)">‹</button><button onclick="movePhoto(${i},1)">›</button></div><input class="sel categoryText" value="${cat}" onchange="setCategoryText(${i},this.value)" onblur="setCategoryText(${i},this.value)" placeholder="facial / focus">${toolButtons(i,p)}</div>`;
08763:       }else html += `<button class="tile empty" onclick="$('fileInput').click()">${i+1}</button>`;
08764:     }
08765:     const grid=$id('uploadGrid'); if(grid) grid.innerHTML=html;
08766:   };
08767:   window.setCategoryText=function(i,v){

08774:   window.runLocalDetect=async function(i){

08780:   };

08807:   function drawContainBox(ctx,img,box,alpha,zoom=1){
08808:     const cw=ctx.canvas.width,ch=ctx.canvas.height;
08809:     const sx=(box?.x||0)*img.naturalWidth, sy=(box?.y||0)*img.naturalHeight, sw=(box?.w||1)*img.naturalWidth, sh=(box?.h||1)*img.naturalHeight;
08810:     const s=Math.min(cw/sw,ch/sh)*zoom;
08811:     const w=sw*s,h=sh*s,x=(cw-w)/2,y=(ch-h)/2;
08812:     ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;ctx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';ctx.drawImage(img,sx,sy,sw,sh,x,y,w,h);ctx.restore();
08813:   }
08814:   function defaultBox(p){
08815:     const cat=parseCat(p?.category,'focus');
08816:     if(p?.landmarks?.mouth && cat==='focus') return expandBoxLocal(p.landmarks.mouth,.55,.70);
08817:     if(p?.landmarks?.face && cat==='facial') return expandBoxLocal(p.landmarks.face,.08,.10);
08818:     if(cat==='facial') return {x:.08,y:.02,w:.84,h:.96};
08819:     return {x:.04,y:.20,w:.92,h:.58};
08820:   }
08821:   function expandBoxLocal(b,px,py){
08822:     const x=clamp(b.x-b.w*px,0,1), y=clamp(b.y-b.h*py,0,1), r=clamp(b.x+b.w*(1+px),0,1), bot=clamp(b.y+b.h*(1+py),0,1);
08823:     return {x,y,w:r-x,h:bot-y};
08824:   }
08825:   const oldDrawMorph=window.drawMorph;
08826:   window.drawMorph=function(t=0){
08827:     const c=$id('morphCanvas'); if(!c){ if(oldDrawMorph)oldDrawMorph(t); return; }
08828:     if(window.sizeCanvas) sizeCanvas();
08829:     const ctx=c.getContext('2d');ctx.clearRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.fillRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
08830:     if(!state.photos.length||!state.imageCache?.length) return;
09354:     p.notes=$id('pmNotes').value.trim();
09355:     p.editState={rotate:ed.rotate||0,flipX:!!ed.flipX,flipY:!!ed.flipY,straighten:ed.straighten||0,zoom:ed.zoom||100,offsetX:ed.offsetX||0,offsetY:ed.offsetY||0};
09356:     const img = await loadImage(ed.baseDataUrl || p.dataUrl);
09357:     const out=document.createElement('canvas'); out.width=img.naturalWidth; out.height=img.naturalHeight;
09358:     const ctx=out.getContext('2d');
09359:     const keepAlpha = String(ed.baseDataUrl||p.dataUrl).startsWith('data:image/png');
09360:     if(!keepAlpha){ ctx.fillStyle='#fff'; ctx.fillRect(0,0,out.width,out.height); }
09361:     ctx.translate(out.width/2 + (ed.offsetX/100)*out.width*.26, out.height/2 + (ed.offsetY/100)*out.height*.26);
09362:     ctx.rotate(((ed.rotate||0)+(ed.straighten||0))*Math.PI/180);
09363:     ctx.scale(ed.flipX?-1:1, ed.flipY?-1:1);
09364:     const zoom=(ed.zoom||100)/100;
09365:     ctx.drawImage(img, -img.naturalWidth*zoom/2, -img.naturalHeight*zoom/2, img.naturalWidth*zoom, img.naturalHeight*zoom);
09366:     p.dataUrl = out.toDataURL(keepAlpha?'image/png':'image/jpeg', .95);
09367:     renderUpload(); if(window.renderPreview) renderPreview(); if(window.renderSimulator) renderSimulator(); closePhotoManager(); toastFn('Photo を更新しました');
09368:   }

09425: <script>
09426: /* ---------- v5.5 manual animation / library / share / nav ---------- */
09427: (function(){
09428:   // `state` is a global lexical `const` (NOT window.state, which isn't assigned until a later
09429:   // script). Guarding on window.state made this whole block early-return, leaving
09430:   // saveCurrentProject/persistLibrary/etc. undefined → "Library に保存" silently failed.
09431:   if(typeof state==='undefined') return;
09432:   if(!window.state){ try{ window.state=state; }catch(e){} }
09433:   const $id=id=>document.getElementById(id);
09434:   const toastFn=msg=>window.toast?toast(msg):console.log(msg);
09435:   state.projectLibrary = state.projectLibrary || [];
09436:   state.manualAnim = state.manualAnim || {frames:[],selected:-1,playing:false,raf:null,playTimer:null,current:{base:-1,overlay:-1,opacity:55,scale:100,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,duration:70,label:''}};
09437:

16349:   function rr(ctx,x,y,w,h,r){ ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x+r,y); ctx.arcTo(x+w,y,x+w,y+h,r); ctx.arcTo(x+w,y+h,x,y+h,r); ctx.arcTo(x,y+h,x,y,r); ctx.arcTo(x,y,x+w,y,r); ctx.closePath(); }

16352:   async function createSimulationImageV82(source){
16353:     const img=await loadImage(source.dataUrl);
16354:     const max=1400, scale=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.naturalWidth||img.width,img.naturalHeight||img.height));
16355:     const w=Math.round((img.naturalWidth||img.width)*scale), h=Math.round((img.naturalHeight||img.height)*scale);
16356:     const c=document.createElement('canvas'); c.width=w; c.height=h;
16357:     const ctx=c.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});
16358:     ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);
16359:     const s=state.simV82, t=s.targets||{}, amount=(s.amount||55)/100;
16360:     const x0=Math.round(w*.10), x1=Math.round(w*.90), bw=x1-x0;
16361:     const yc=Math.round(h*(clamp(s.band||50,25,75)/100));
16362:     const bh=Math.round(h*.30), y0=Math.max(0,Math.round(yc-bh/2)), y1=Math.min(h,Math.round(yc+bh/2)), bhh=y1-y0;
16363:     const shade=(t.shade?1:0)*amount*((s.shade||46)/55);
16364:     const squeeze=(t.space?1:0)*amount*.055;
16365:     const scallop=(t.scallop?1:0)*amount*((s.scallop||52)/55);
16366:     const incisal=(t.incisal?1:0)*amount;
16367:     const harmony=(t.harmony?1:0)*amount;
16368:     const missing=(t.missing?1:0)*amount*((s.missing||30)/55);
16369: 
16370:     if(squeeze>0 && bw>4 && bhh>4){
16371:       const tmp=document.createElement('canvas'); tmp.width=bw; tmp.height=bhh;
16372:       tmp.getContext('2d').drawImage(c,x0,y0,bw,bhh,0,0,bw,bhh);
16373:       const nw=bw*(1-squeeze); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha=.72; ctx.drawImage(tmp,0,0,bw,bhh,x0+(bw-nw)/2,y0,nw,bhh); ctx.restore();
16374:     }
16375:     if(shade>0 && bw>0 && bhh>0){
16376:       const id=ctx.getImageData(x0,y0,bw,bhh), d=id.data;
16377:       for(let i=0;i<d.length;i+=4){
16378:         const r=d[i],g=d[i+1],b=d[i+2]; const l=(r+g+b)/3, ch=Math.max(r,g,b)-Math.min(r,g,b);
16379:         if(l>92 && ch<125){
16380:           const k=shade*Math.min(1,(l-82)/160);
16381:           d[i]=Math.min(255,r+(255-r)*k*.58); d[i+1]=Math.min(255,g+(252-g)*k*.64); d[i+2]=Math.min(255,b+(246-b)*k*.74);
16382:         }
16383:       }
16384:       ctx.putImageData(id,x0,y0);
16385:     }
16386:     if(scallop>0){
16387:       ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha=Math.min(.38,.10+scallop*.30); ctx.fillStyle='#d99098';
16388:       const gy=y0+bhh*.10, N=8;
16389:       for(let i=1;i<N;i++){ const x=x0+bw*i/N; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x-6,gy); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, gy+10+scallop*4, x+6, gy); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); }
16390:       ctx.restore();
16391:     }
16198:     // Auto-pick from gallery only if the user has NOT explicitly dismissed this slot.

16201:     const micro = state.simMicro || (state._simMicroExcluded ? null : pickCategoryPhoto('focus'));

16212:       stateEl.textContent = macro ? 'Facial image ready' : 'No image selected';

16245:           .catch(()=>{ micro.intraoral=false; });
16246:       }

16251:     // the v8.2 "Facial image ready / Focus image ready" display.
16252:     [macroCard, microCard].forEach(card=>{
16253:       if(!card) return;

16348:   function loadImage(src){ return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{ const img=new Image(); img.onload=()=>resolve(img); img.onerror=reject; img.src=src; }); }
16349:   function rr(ctx,x,y,w,h,r){ ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x+r,y); ctx.arcTo(x+w,y,x+w,y+h,r); ctx.arcTo(x+w,y+h,x,y+h,r); ctx.arcTo(x,y+h,x,y,r); ctx.arcTo(x,y,x+w,y,r); ctx.closePath(); }
16350:   function drawMark(ctx,w,h){ ctx.save(); const fs=Math.max(16,Math.round(w*.025)); ctx.font=`900 ${fs}px -apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,"Helvetica Neue",Arial,sans-serif`; ctx.textAlign='right'; ctx.textBaseline='bottom'; ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.92)'; ctx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.20)'; ctx.shadowBlur=10; ctx.fillText('CASEFLOW STUDIO™',w-Math.max(18,w*.035),h-Math.max(18,h*.035)); ctx.restore(); }

16352:   async function createSimulationImageV82(source){
16353:     const img=await loadImage(source.dataUrl);
16354:     const max=1400, scale=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.naturalWidth||img.width,img.naturalHeight||img.height));
16355:     const w=Math.round((img.naturalWidth||img.width)*scale), h=Math.round((img.naturalHeight||img.height)*scale);
16356:     const c=document.createElement('canvas'); c.width=w; c.height=h;
16357:     const ctx=c.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});
16358:     ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);
16359:     const s=state.simV82, t=s.targets||{}, amount=(s.amount||55)/100;
16360:     const x0=Math.round(w*.10), x1=Math.round(w*.90), bw=x1-x0;
16361:     const yc=Math.round(h*(clamp(s.band||50,25,75)/100));
16362:     const bh=Math.round(h*.30), y0=Math.max(0,Math.round(yc-bh/2)), y1=Math.min(h,Math.round(yc+bh/2)), bhh=y1-y0;
16363:     const shade=(t.shade?1:0)*amount*((s.shade||46)/55);
16364:     const squeeze=(t.space?1:0)*amount*.055;
16365:     const scallop=(t.scallop?1:0)*amount*((s.scallop||52)/55);
16366:     const incisal=(t.incisal?1:0)*amount;
16367:     const harmony=(t.harmony?1:0)*amount;
16368:     const missing=(t.missing?1:0)*amount*((s.missing||30)/55);
16369: 
16370:     if(squeeze>0 && bw>4 && bhh>4){
16371:       const tmp=document.createElement('canvas'); tmp.width=bw; tmp.height=bhh;
16372:       tmp.getContext('2d').drawImage(c,x0,y0,bw,bhh,0,0,bw,bhh);
16373:       const nw=bw*(1-squeeze); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha=.72; ctx.drawImage(tmp,0,0,bw,bhh,x0+(bw-nw)/2,y0,nw,bhh); ctx.restore();
16374:     }
16375:     if(shade>0 && bw>0 && bhh>0){
16376:       const id=ctx.getImageData(x0,y0,bw,bhh), d=id.data;
16377:       for(let i=0;i<d.length;i+=4){
16378:         const r=d[i],g=d[i+1],b=d[i+2]; const l=(r+g+b)/3, ch=Math.max(r,g,b)-Math.min(r,g,b);
16379:         if(l>92 && ch<125){
16380:           const k=shade*Math.min(1,(l-82)/160);
16381:           d[i]=Math.min(255,r+(255-r)*k*.58); d[i+1]=Math.min(255,g+(252-g)*k*.64); d[i+2]=Math.min(255,b+(246-b)*k*.74);
16382:         }
16383:       }
16384:       ctx.putImageData(id,x0,y0);
16385:     }
16386:     if(scallop>0){
16387:       ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha=Math.min(.38,.10+scallop*.30); ctx.fillStyle='#d99098';
16388:       const gy=y0+bhh*.10, N=8;
16389:       for(let i=1;i<N;i++){ const x=x0+bw*i/N; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x-6,gy); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, gy+10+scallop*4, x+6, gy); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); }
16390:       ctx.restore();
16391:     }
16392:     if(missing>0){
16241:       // highlights the auto-detected type.
16242:       if(micro && typeof micro.intraoral !== 'boolean' && window.caseflowLocalAnalyze){
16243:         window.caseflowLocalAnalyze(micro.dataUrl, micro.category||'focus')
16244:           .then(a=>{ micro.intraoral=!!(a&&a.intraoral); micro.intraoralDark=!!(a&&a.intraoralDark); if(typeof micro.lips!=='boolean') micro.lips=!micro.intraoral; updateSimulationChoicesV82(); })
16245:           .catch(()=>{ micro.intraoral=false; });
16246:       }
16247:     }
16248:     ensureSimCardActions(macroCard,'facial',macro);
16249:     ensureSimCardActions(microCard,'focus',micro);
16250:     // Remove legacy v6.6 "Not selected / Using selected image" badge that conflicts with
16251:     // the v8.2 "Facial image ready / Focus image ready" display.
16252:     [macroCard, microCard].forEach(card=>{
16253:       if(!card) return;
16254:       card.querySelector('.simChoiceState')?.remove();
16255:       // Also hide the original subtitle span that v6.6 overwrites with "未選択..." text,
16256:       // since v8.2 has .choiceFileMeta + .choiceState for the same info.
16257:       const inner=card.querySelector('div');
16258:       const origSpan=inner?.querySelector('span:not(.choiceFileMeta):not(.choiceState)');
16259:       if(origSpan) origSpan.style.display='none';
16260:     });
16261:     // Gate generate button: if a Micro image is loaded but shot type not yet confirmed, block.
16262:     const genBtn=document.querySelector('#simulator button.primary[onclick*="generateSimulation"]');
16263:     if(genBtn){
16264:       const unconfirmed=!!(micro&&!micro._shotConfirmed);
16265:       genBtn.disabled=unconfirmed;
16266:       genBtn.title=unconfirmed?'撮影タイプ（必須）を選択してください':'';
16267:     }

16348:   function loadImage(src){ return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{ const img=new Image(); img.onload=()=>resolve(img); img.onerror=reject; img.src=src; }); }
16349:   function rr(ctx,x,y,w,h,r){ ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x+r,y); ctx.arcTo(x+w,y,x+w,y+h,r); ctx.arcTo(x+w,y+h,x,y+h,r); ctx.arcTo(x,y+h,x,y,r); ctx.arcTo(x,y,x+w,y,r); ctx.closePath(); }
16350:   function drawMark(ctx,w,h){ ctx.save(); const fs=Math.max(16,Math.round(w*.025)); ctx.font=`900 ${fs}px -apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,"Helvetica Neue",Arial,sans-serif`; ctx.textAlign='right'; ctx.textBaseline='bottom'; ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.92)'; ctx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.20)'; ctx.shadowBlur=10; ctx.fillText('CASEFLOW STUDIO™',w-Math.max(18,w*.035),h-Math.max(18,h*.035)); ctx.restore(); }

16352:   async function createSimulationImageV82(source){
16353:     const img=await loadImage(source.dataUrl);
16354:     const max=1400, scale=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.naturalWidth||img.width,img.naturalHeight||img.height));
16355:     const w=Math.round((img.naturalWidth||img.width)*scale), h=Math.round((img.naturalHeight||img.height)*scale);
16356:     const c=document.createElement('canvas'); c.width=w; c.height=h;
16357:     const ctx=c.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});
16358:     ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);
16359:     const s=state.simV82, t=s.targets||{}, amount=(s.amount||55)/100;
16360:     const x0=Math.round(w*.10), x1=Math.round(w*.90), bw=x1-x0;
16361:     const yc=Math.round(h*(clamp(s.band||50,25,75)/100));
16362:     const bh=Math.round(h*.30), y0=Math.max(0,Math.round(yc-bh/2)), y1=Math.min(h,Math.round(yc+bh/2)), bhh=y1-y0;
16363:     const shade=(t.shade?1:0)*amount*((s.shade||46)/55);
16364:     const squeeze=(t.space?1:0)*amount*.055;
16365:     const scallop=(t.scallop?1:0)*amount*((s.scallop||52)/55);
16366:     const incisal=(t.incisal?1:0)*amount;
16367:     const harmony=(t.harmony?1:0)*amount;
16368:     const missing=(t.missing?1:0)*amount*((s.missing||30)/55);
16369: 
16370:     if(squeeze>0 && bw>4 && bhh>4){
16371:       const tmp=document.createElement('canvas'); tmp.width=bw; tmp.height=bhh;
16372:       tmp.getContext('2d').drawImage(c,x0,y0,bw,bhh,0,0,bw,bhh);
16373:       const nw=bw*(1-squeeze); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha=.72; ctx.drawImage(tmp,0,0,bw,bhh,x0+(bw-nw)/2,y0,nw,bhh); ctx.restore();
16374:     }
16375:     if(shade>0 && bw>0 && bhh>0){
16376:       const id=ctx.getImageData(x0,y0,bw,bhh), d=id.data;
16377:       for(let i=0;i<d.length;i+=4){
16378:         const r=d[i],g=d[i+1],b=d[i+2]; const l=(r+g+b)/3, ch=Math.max(r,g,b)-Math.min(r,g,b);
16379:         if(l>92 && ch<125){
16380:           const k=shade*Math.min(1,(l-82)/160);
16381:           d[i]=Math.min(255,r+(255-r)*k*.58); d[i+1]=Math.min(255,g+(252-g)*k*.64); d[i+2]=Math.min(255,b+(246-b)*k*.74);
16382:         }
16383:       }
16384:       ctx.putImageData(id,x0,y0);
16385:     }
16386:     if(scallop>0){
16387:       ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha=Math.min(.38,.10+scallop*.30); ctx.fillStyle='#d99098';
16388:       const gy=y0+bhh*.10, N=8;
16389:       for(let i=1;i<N;i++){ const x=x0+bw*i/N; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x-6,gy); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, gy+10+scallop*4, x+6, gy); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); }
16390:       ctx.restore();
16391:     }
16392:     if(missing>0){
16393:       ctx.save();
16394:       ctx.globalAlpha=Math.min(.42,.10+missing*.28);
16395:       ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.86)';
16396:       const rx=w*.60, ry=yc-h*.015, rw=w*.085, rh=h*.12;
16397:       rr(ctx,rx,ry,rw,rh,Math.max(12,w*.01)); ctx.fill();
16398:       ctx.globalAlpha=.18+missing*.16;
16399:       ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(32,27,53,.22)'; ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2,w*.0032); rr(ctx,rx,ry,rw,rh,Math.max(12,w*.01)); ctx.stroke();
16400:       ctx.restore();
16401:     }
16402:     if(harmony>0){
16403:       ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha=.10+harmony*.16; ctx.fillStyle='#fff'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(w*.5,yc,w*.33,h*.045,0,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
16404:       ctx.globalAlpha=.12+harmony*.14; ctx.strokeStyle='#fff'; ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2,w*.0035); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(w*.26,yc+h*.042); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(w*.5,yc+h*.076,w*.74,yc+h*.042); ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore();
16405:     }
16406:     if(incisal>0){
16407:       ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha=.20+incisal*.22; ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.92)'; ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2,w*.004); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(w*.30,yc+h*.055); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(w*.5,yc+h*.082,w*.70,yc+h*.055); ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore();
16408:     }
16409:     ctx.save(); ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.84)'; rr(ctx,w*.035,h*.035,w*.50,Math.max(44,h*.062),22); ctx.fill();
16410:     ctx.fillStyle='#201b35'; ctx.font=`900 ${Math.max(16,Math.round(w/38))}px -apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,sans-serif`; ctx.fillText('Simulation / Reference',w*.055,h*.075);
16411:     ctx.fillStyle='rgba(32,27,53,.55)'; ctx.font=`760 ${Math.max(10,Math.round(w/80))}px -apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,sans-serif`; ctx.fillText('For explanation only',w*.055,h*.103); ctx.restore();
16412:     drawMark(ctx,w,h);
16413:     return c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',.93);
16414:   }

16416:   window.generateSimulation = async function(){
16417:     const source=state.simMicro || state.simMacro || pickCategoryPhoto('focus') || pickCategoryPhoto('facial') || (state.photos||[])[0];
16418:     if(!source){ toastFn('Simulation Studio で使用する画像をアップロードしてください'); return; }
16419:     state._simSource = source;
16420:     toastFn('Reference Image を作成中…');
16421:     try{
16422:       const result=await createSimulationImageV82(source);
16423:       state.simResult = { dataUrl:result, source:source.dataUrl, sourceKind:(source.category==='facial'?'Facial':source.category==='focus'?'Focus':'Image'), createdAt:new Date().toISOString(), settings:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.simV82||{})) };
16424:       if(typeof go==='function') go('result');
16425:       if(typeof renderResult==='function') setTimeout(()=>renderResult(),80);
16426:     }catch(err){ console.error(err); toastFn('画像の作成に失敗しました'); }
16427:   };

16431:     if(prevRenderSimulator) prevRenderSimulator();

16433:     bindSimulationUploadsV82();
16434:     const primary=document.querySelector('#simulator button.primary[onclick*="generateSimulation"]');
16435:     if(primary) primary.innerHTML='Reference Image 作成';
16253:       if(!card) return;
16254:       card.querySelector('.simChoiceState')?.remove();
16255:       // Also hide the original subtitle span that v6.6 overwrites with "未選択..." text,
16256:       // since v8.2 has .choiceFileMeta + .choiceState for the same info.
16257:       const inner=card.querySelector('div');
16258:       const origSpan=inner?.querySelector('span:not(.choiceFileMeta):not(.choiceState)');
16259:       if(origSpan) origSpan.style.display='none';
16260:     });
16261:     // Gate generate button: if a Micro image is loaded but shot type not yet confirmed, block.
16262:     const genBtn=document.querySelector('#simulator button.primary[onclick*="generateSimulation"]');
16263:     if(genBtn){
16264:       const unconfirmed=!!(micro&&!micro._shotConfirmed);
16265:       genBtn.disabled=unconfirmed;
16266:       genBtn.title=unconfirmed?'撮影タイプ（必須）を選択してください':'';
16267:     }

16270:   async function resizeAdvancedLocal(file){
16271:     if(typeof resizeAdvanced==='function') return await resizeAdvanced(file);
16272:     if(typeof resize==='function') return await resize(file);
16273:     return await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const fr=new FileReader(); fr.onload=()=>resolve(fr.result); fr.onerror=reject; fr.readAsDataURL(file);});
16274:   }

16276:   function bindSimulationUploadsV82(){
16277:     const oldMacro=$id('simMacroInput'), oldMicro=$id('simMicroInput');
16278:     if(!oldMacro||!oldMicro) return;
16279:     const macroInput=oldMacro.cloneNode(true), microInput=oldMicro.cloneNode(true);
16280:     oldMacro.parentNode.replaceChild(macroInput, oldMacro);
16281:     oldMicro.parentNode.replaceChild(microInput, oldMicro);
16282:     macroInput.addEventListener('change', async e=>{
16283:       const f=e.target.files && e.target.files[0]; if(!f) return;
16284:       const dataUrl=await resizeAdvancedLocal(f);
16285:       let intra=false, dark=false, lm=null, aMac=null;
16286:       try{ if(window.caseflowLocalAnalyze){ aMac=await window.caseflowLocalAnalyze(dataUrl,'facial'); intra=!!aMac.intraoral; dark=!!aMac.intraoralDark; lm=aMac.landmarks; } }catch(_){}
16287:       // Facial portraits inherently have lips — no shot-type picker needed on this card.
16288:       state._simMacroExcluded=false;
16289:       state.simMacro={dataUrl, label:'Macro / Facial', category:'facial', intraoral:intra, intraoralDark:dark, lips:!intra, _shotConfirmed:true, macroHasBrackets:false, landmarks:lm, name:f.name||'', date:new Date().toISOString()};
16290:       updateSimulationChoicesV82();
16291:       toastFn('Facial image を設定しました');
16292:       e.target.value='';
16293:     });
16294:     microInput.addEventListener('change', async e=>{
16295:       const f=e.target.files && e.target.files[0]; if(!f) return;
16296:       const dataUrl=await resizeAdvancedLocal(f);
16297:       let a=null;
16298:       try{ if(window.caseflowLocalAnalyze) a=await window.caseflowLocalAnalyze(dataUrl,'focus'); }catch(_){}
16299:       // No popup at upload: seed flags from auto-detection; the shot-type picker inside the
16300:       // Micro/Focus card is where the user confirms / corrects リップの有無・コントラスター.
16301:       const intra=!!(a&&a.intraoral), dark=!!(a&&a.intraoralDark);
16302:       state._simMicroExcluded=false;
16303:       state.simMicro={dataUrl, label:'Micro / Focus', category:'focus', intraoral:intra, intraoralDark:dark, lips:!intra, landmarks:a?a.landmarks:null, name:f.name||'', date:new Date().toISOString()};
16304:       updateSimulationChoicesV82();
16305:       toastFn('Focus image を設定しました');
16306:       e.target.value='';
16307:     });
16308:     const macroCard=$id('macroChoice'), microCard=$id('microChoice');
16309:     if(macroCard) macroCard.onclick=()=>macroInput.click();
16310:     if(microCard) microCard.onclick=()=>microInput.click();
16311:     updateSimulationChoicesV82();
16312:   }

20596:   function renderResultImages(){
20597:     // Seed the gallery from a legacy single simResult (e.g. loaded project).
20598:     if((!Array.isArray(state.simResults)||!state.simResults.length) && state.simResult && state.simResult.dataUrl){
20599:       state.simResults=[state.simResult]; state.simResultIndex=0;
20600:     }
20601:     const arr=Array.isArray(state.simResults)?state.simResults:[];
20602:     const r=state.simResult || arr[state.simResultIndex||0], src=state._simSource||pickSource();
20603:     if(!r||!src){ renderSimGallery(); return; }
20604:     const before=$id('resultBefore'), after=$id('resultAfter');
20605:     // ⑤ ALWAYS pair the before image with THIS result's OWN source (never the global
20606:     // _simSource, which is stale when switching between variations generated from different
20607:     // uploads). r.source / r.srcObj are stored per-result in pushSimResult/regenerate.
20608:     const beforeSrc=(r.source) || (r.srcObj && r.srcObj.dataUrl) || src.dataUrl;
20609:     if(before) before.src=beforeSrc;
20610:     if(after)  after.src=r.dataUrl;
20611:     const cap=$id('resultCaption');
20612:     if(cap) cap.textContent=`${sourceKindLabel(src)} image / Reference Image — 説明用の参考イメージであり、治療結果の保証ではありません。`;
20613:     if(typeof setupCompareSlider==='function'){ const el=$id('simulationCompareSlider'); if(el) setupCompareSlider(el); }
20614:     renderSimGallery();

20620:     let tag=$id('simV96Busy');

20626:   // Guarantee the contraster intraoral flag is resolved before we generate, regardless of
20627:   // which upload path created the source object. Mutates the source in place (and thus the
20628:   // shared _simSource reference) so the enamel mask + caption pick it up.
20629:   /* CF_PRIMARY_HARD_DEADLINE_V3 */
20630:   async function ensureIntraoralFlag(src){
20631:     if(!src || !src.dataUrl) return false;
20632:     const resolveLips=()=>{
20633:       if(typeof src.lips!=='boolean') src.lips=(((src.category||'').toLowerCase()==='facial') && !src.intraoral);
20634:     };
20635:     if(typeof src.intraoral==='boolean' && typeof src.intraoralDark==='boolean'){ resolveLips(); return src.intraoral; }
20636:     try{
20637:       if(window.caseflowLocalAnalyze){
20638:         const timeoutToken={cfTimeout:true};
20639:         const a=await Promise.race([
20640:           window.caseflowLocalAnalyze(src.dataUrl,src.category||null),
20641:           new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(()=>resolve(timeoutToken),650))
20642:         ]);
20643:         if(a!==timeoutToken){
20644:           src.intraoral=!!(a&&a.intraoral); src.intraoralDark=!!(a&&a.intraoralDark);
20645:           resolveLips(); return src.intraoral;
20646:         }
20647:       }
20648:     }catch(e){}
20649:     // Generation may never wait for analysis. Category and upload controls are the safe default.
20650:     if(typeof src.intraoral!=='boolean') src.intraoral=((src.category||'').toLowerCase()==='intraoral');
20651:     if(typeof src.intraoralDark!=='boolean') src.intraoralDark=false;
20652:     resolveLips(); return src.intraoral;
20653:   }
20654:   window.caseflowEnsureIntraoral=ensureIntraoralFlag;
20655:   // Produce the after-image: photoreal AI backend when enabled+configured, else the local
20656:   // artifact-free colour/finish pass.
20657:   const CF_PRIMARY_RESULT_DEADLINE_MS=18000;
20658:   const CF_PRIMARY_PREFLIGHT_MS=700;
20659:   async function cfFastPrimaryPreview(src,raw){
20660:     if(!src||!src.dataUrl) return null;
20661:     try{
20662:       const img=await Promise.race([
20663:         loadImage(src.dataUrl),
20664:         new Promise((_,reject)=>setTimeout(()=>reject(new Error('quick preview decode timeout')),1800))
20665:       ]);
20666:       const iw=img.naturalWidth||img.width,ih=img.naturalHeight||img.height;
20667:       if(!iw||!ih) return src.dataUrl;
20668:       const maxEdge=960,scale=Math.min(1,maxEdge/Math.max(iw,ih));
20669:       const w=Math.max(1,Math.round(iw*scale)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(ih*scale));
20670:       const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=w;c.height=h;
20671:       const x=c.getContext('2d',{alpha:false});x.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;x.imageSmoothingQuality='high';x.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);
20672:       // A deliberately conservative, GPU-light optical preview. It never claims structural AI accuracy.
20673:       const facial=((src.category||'').toLowerCase()==='facial')||(!src.intraoral&&src.lips!==false);
20674:       const rx=facial?w*.31:w*.12, ry=facial?h*.50:h*.18, rw=facial?w*.38:w*.76, rh=facial?h*.23:h*.55;
20675:       const tmp=document.createElement('canvas');tmp.width=w;tmp.height=h;
20676:       const tx=tmp.getContext('2d');
20677:       const shade=((raw&&raw.shade)!=null?raw.shade:50); const bright=1+Math.max(0,shade-50)/50*.07;
20678:       tx.filter='brightness('+bright.toFixed(3)+') saturate(.92) contrast(1.025)';tx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);tx.filter='none';
20679:       x.save();x.beginPath();x.ellipse(rx+rw/2,ry+rh/2,rw/2,rh/2,0,0,Math.PI*2);x.clip();x.globalAlpha=.74;x.drawImage(tmp,0,0);x.restore();
20680:       try{drawBadge(x,w,h);}catch(e){}
20681:       const out=c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',.82);c.width=c.height=1;tmp.width=tmp.height=1;return out;
20682:     }catch(e){ console.warn('[CaseFlow] quick preview fallback uses source',e); return src.dataUrl; }
20683:   }
20684:   window.cfFastPrimaryPreview=cfFastPrimaryPreview;
20685:   async function produceImage(src){
20686:     const started=performance.now();
20687:     let progressTimer=null, deadlineTimer=null;
20688:     window.__caseflowQuickPreviewUsed=false;
20689:     try{sessionStorage.setItem('cf_primary_generation_guard_v3',JSON.stringify({startedAt:Date.now()}));}catch(e){}
20690:     try{if(window.cfWait)cfWait.show('AIシミュレーション中',0);}catch(e){}
20691:     const quickTask=cfFastPrimaryPreview(src,state.simV96||{});
20692:     try{
20693:       await Promise.allSettled([
20694:         Promise.race([ensureIntraoralFlag(src),new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,CF_PRIMARY_PREFLIGHT_MS))]),
20695:         Promise.race([ensureProblemList(src),new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,CF_PRIMARY_PREFLIGHT_MS))])
20696:       ]);
20697:     }catch(e){}
20698:     const preflightMs=Math.round(performance.now()-started);
20699:     const aiOn=aiEnabled();
20700:     if(aiOn){
20701:       const msgs=['画像を最適化中…','AIシミュレーション生成中…','臨床デザインを確認中…'];let mi=0;
20702:       const tick=()=>{try{const q=document.querySelector('.cfWaitOverlay .cfWaitSub');if(q)q.textContent=msgs[mi++%msgs.length];}catch(e){}};
20703:       tick();progressTimer=setInterval(tick,2200);
20704:     }
20705:     try{
20706:       if(aiOn){
20707:         const aiTask=generateViaAI(src).then(url=>({kind:'ai',url})).catch(err=>({kind:'error',err}));
20708:         const remaining=Math.max(2500,CF_PRIMARY_RESULT_DEADLINE_MS-(performance.now()-started));
20709:         const deadlineTask=new Promise(resolve=>{deadlineTimer=setTimeout(()=>resolve({kind:'deadline'}),remaining);});
20710:         const result=await Promise.race([aiTask,deadlineTask]);
20711:         if(result&&result.kind==='ai'&&result.url){
20712:           window.__caseflowPrimaryResultMode='ai';return result.url;
20713:         }
20714:         if(result&&result.kind==='error'&&result.err&&result.err.cfNoTokens) throw result.err;
20715:         if(result&&result.kind==='deadline'){try{if(window.__cfGenerationAbort)window.__cfGenerationAbort();}catch(e){}}
20716:         if(result&&result.kind==='error') window.__caseflowLastAIError=String((result.err&&result.err.message)||result.err||'AI error');
20717:       }
20718:       // Absolute contract: always resolve the first result screen without invoking the heavy
20719:       // full-frame local pixel/MediaPipe fallback after an AI stall.
20720:       let quick=null;
20721:       try{quick=await Promise.race([quickTask,new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(()=>resolve(src&&src.dataUrl||null),1200))]);}catch(e){quick=src&&src.dataUrl||null;}
20722:       if(!quick) throw new Error('一次プレビューを作成できませんでした');
20723:       window.__caseflowQuickPreviewUsed=true;window.__caseflowPrimaryResultMode='quick';
20724:       setTimeout(()=>{try{toastFn('通信が時間内に完了しなかったため、軽量プレビューを表示しました。精密生成は結果画面から再実行できます。');}catch(e){}},80);
20725:       return quick;
20726:     } finally {
20727:       clearInterval(progressTimer);clearTimeout(deadlineTimer);
20728:       const totalMs=Math.round(performance.now()-started);
20729:       window.__caseflowGenerationTiming={preflight_ms:preflightMs,total_ms:totalMs,mode:window.__caseflowPrimaryResultMode||'unknown',hard_deadline_ms:CF_PRIMARY_RESULT_DEADLINE_MS,completed_at:new Date().toISOString()};
20730:       try{if(typeof window.cfLogEvent==='function')window.cfLogEvent('generation_timing','visual-simulation',window.__caseflowGenerationTiming);}catch(e){}
20731:       try{sessionStorage.removeItem('cf_primary_generation_guard_v3');}catch(e){}
20732:       try{if(window.cfWait)cfWait.hideAll();}catch(e){}
20733:     }
20734:   }
20735:   window.caseflowProduceSimImage=produceImage;

20737:   async function regenerate(){
20738:     if(regenBusy){ regenQueued=true; return; }
20739:     const src=state._simSource||pickSource();
20740:     if(!src) return;
20741:     regenBusy=true; setBusy(true);
20742:     try{
20743:       const url=await produceImage(src);
20744:       // Slider edits UPDATE the currently selected gallery slot in place (other variations
20745:       // stay intact), so re-editing never makes a previously generated image disappear.
20746:       const r=Object.assign({}, state.simResult, {
20747:         dataUrl:url, source:src.dataUrl, srcObj:src, sourceKind:sourceKind(src),
20748:         createdAt:new Date().toISOString(), settings:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.simV96))
20749:       });
20750:       if(Array.isArray(state.simResults) && state.simResults.length){ state.simResults[state.simResultIndex||0]=r; }
20751:       else { state.simResults=[r]; state.simResultIndex=0; }
20752:       state.simResult=r;
20753:       cfAutoSaveTempSim(r);   // ① keep the 48h temp Library copy in sync with slider edits
20754:       renderResultImages();
20755:       // Persist the new result immediately so a reload won't lose it
20756:       try{ if(window.cfPersist) cfPersist.schedule(1000); }catch(e){}
20757:     }catch(e){ console.error(e); toastFn('再生成に失敗しました'); }
20758:     regenBusy=false; setBusy(false);
20759:     if(regenQueued){ regenQueued=false; regenerate(); }
20760:   }
20761:   function scheduleRegen(delay){
20762:     clearTimeout(regenTimer);
20763:     regenTimer=setTimeout(regenerate, delay==null?60:delay);
20764:   }

20685:   async function produceImage(src){
20686:     const started=performance.now();
20687:     let progressTimer=null, deadlineTimer=null;
20688:     window.__caseflowQuickPreviewUsed=false;
20689:     try{sessionStorage.setItem('cf_primary_generation_guard_v3',JSON.stringify({startedAt:Date.now()}));}catch(e){}
20690:     try{if(window.cfWait)cfWait.show('AIシミュレーション中',0);}catch(e){}
20691:     const quickTask=cfFastPrimaryPreview(src,state.simV96||{});
20692:     try{
20693:       await Promise.allSettled([
20694:         Promise.race([ensureIntraoralFlag(src),new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,CF_PRIMARY_PREFLIGHT_MS))]),
20695:         Promise.race([ensureProblemList(src),new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,CF_PRIMARY_PREFLIGHT_MS))])
20696:       ]);
20697:     }catch(e){}
20698:     const preflightMs=Math.round(performance.now()-started);
20699:     const aiOn=aiEnabled();
20700:     if(aiOn){
20701:       const msgs=['画像を最適化中…','AIシミュレーション生成中…','臨床デザインを確認中…'];let mi=0;
20702:       const tick=()=>{try{const q=document.querySelector('.cfWaitOverlay .cfWaitSub');if(q)q.textContent=msgs[mi++%msgs.length];}catch(e){}};
20703:       tick();progressTimer=setInterval(tick,2200);
20704:     }
20705:     try{
20706:       if(aiOn){
20707:         const aiTask=generateViaAI(src).then(url=>({kind:'ai',url})).catch(err=>({kind:'error',err}));
20708:         const remaining=Math.max(2500,CF_PRIMARY_RESULT_DEADLINE_MS-(performance.now()-started));
20709:         const deadlineTask=new Promise(resolve=>{deadlineTimer=setTimeout(()=>resolve({kind:'deadline'}),remaining);});
20710:         const result=await Promise.race([aiTask,deadlineTask]);
20711:         if(result&&result.kind==='ai'&&result.url){
20712:           window.__caseflowPrimaryResultMode='ai';return result.url;
20713:         }
20714:         if(result&&result.kind==='error'&&result.err&&result.err.cfNoTokens) throw result.err;
20715:         if(result&&result.kind==='deadline'){try{if(window.__cfGenerationAbort)window.__cfGenerationAbort();}catch(e){}}
20716:         if(result&&result.kind==='error') window.__caseflowLastAIError=String((result.err&&result.err.message)||result.err||'AI error');
20717:       }
20718:       // Absolute contract: always resolve the first result screen without invoking the heavy
20719:       // full-frame local pixel/MediaPipe fallback after an AI stall.
20720:       let quick=null;
20721:       try{quick=await Promise.race([quickTask,new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(()=>resolve(src&&src.dataUrl||null),1200))]);}catch(e){quick=src&&src.dataUrl||null;}
20722:       if(!quick) throw new Error('一次プレビューを作成できませんでした');
20723:       window.__caseflowQuickPreviewUsed=true;window.__caseflowPrimaryResultMode='quick';
20724:       setTimeout(()=>{try{toastFn('通信が時間内に完了しなかったため、軽量プレビューを表示しました。精密生成は結果画面から再実行できます。');}catch(e){}},80);
20725:       return quick;
20726:     } finally {
20727:       clearInterval(progressTimer);clearTimeout(deadlineTimer);
20728:       const totalMs=Math.round(performance.now()-started);
20729:       window.__caseflowGenerationTiming={preflight_ms:preflightMs,total_ms:totalMs,mode:window.__caseflowPrimaryResultMode||'unknown',hard_deadline_ms:CF_PRIMARY_RESULT_DEADLINE_MS,completed_at:new Date().toISOString()};
20730:       try{if(typeof window.cfLogEvent==='function')window.cfLogEvent('generation_timing','visual-simulation',window.__caseflowGenerationTiming);}catch(e){}

20737:   async function regenerate(){
20738:     if(regenBusy){ regenQueued=true; return; }
20739:     const src=state._simSource||pickSource();
20740:     if(!src) return;
20741:     regenBusy=true; setBusy(true);
20742:     try{
20743:       const url=await produceImage(src);
20744:       // Slider edits UPDATE the currently selected gallery slot in place (other variations
20745:       // stay intact), so re-editing never makes a previously generated image disappear.
20746:       const r=Object.assign({}, state.simResult, {
20747:         dataUrl:url, source:src.dataUrl, srcObj:src, sourceKind:sourceKind(src),
20748:         createdAt:new Date().toISOString(), settings:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.simV96))
20749:       });
20750:       if(Array.isArray(state.simResults) && state.simResults.length){ state.simResults[state.simResultIndex||0]=r; }
20751:       else { state.simResults=[r]; state.simResultIndex=0; }
20752:       state.simResult=r;
20753:       cfAutoSaveTempSim(r);   // ① keep the 48h temp Library copy in sync with slider edits
20754:       renderResultImages();
20755:       // Persist the new result immediately so a reload won't lose it
20756:       try{ if(window.cfPersist) cfPersist.schedule(1000); }catch(e){}
20757:     }catch(e){ console.error(e); toastFn('再生成に失敗しました'); }
20758:     regenBusy=false; setBusy(false);
20759:     if(regenQueued){ regenQueued=false; regenerate(); }
20760:   }

06351:   async function callAISimulation(){
06352:     const source = state.simMicro || state.simMacro || state.photos.find(p=>p.category==='focus') || state.photos[0];
06353:     if(!source){ toast('先に Macro / Facial と Micro / Focus を設定してください'); return false; }
06354:     toast('AI Simulation backend に接続中…');
06355:     try{
06356:       const res = await fetch('/api/ai_simulation',{
06357:         method:'POST',
08636:     //     upload-time popup acts as the manual safety net for the remaining edge cases).
08637:     const intraoralDark = (darkBorderRatio>0.34 || bgBright<92) && mouthRatio>0.18;
08638:     // (B) Retracted intraoral shot (cheek retractors, NO black contraster): the frame is
08639:     //     filled with oral content (teeth + gingiva), there is essentially no facial skin
08640:     //     spread, and it is a wide (landscape) close-up rather than a tall portrait. This
08641:     //     catches ordinary retracted intraoral photos that have a normal (non-black) bg.
08642:     const faceArea = faceBox ? faceBox.w*faceBox.h : 0;
08643:     const noFace = faceArea < 0.22;
08644:     const landscape = img.naturalWidth >= img.naturalHeight*0.95;
08645:     const toothFrac = whiteInside/(W*H);
08646:     const darkFrac  = darkCount/(W*H);
08647:     const retracted = mouthRatio>0.36 && noFace && landscape && toothFrac>0.015 && darkFrac>0.02;
08648:     const intraoral = intraoralDark || retracted;
08649:     if(intraoral && !forcedCategory) category='focus';   // any intraoral close-up is Focus
08650:     const mouth = lipBox || (category==='facial' ? {x:contentBox.x+contentBox.w*.30,y:contentBox.y+contentBox.h*.55,w:contentBox.w*.40,h:contentBox.h*.18} : {x:contentBox.x+contentBox.w*.08,y:contentBox.y+contentBox.h*.28,w:contentBox.w*.84,h:contentBox.h*.44});
08651:     const teethFinal = teethBox || {x:mouth.x+mouth.w*.18,y:mouth.y+mouth.h*.33,w:mouth.w*.64,h:mouth.h*.32};
08652:     return {
08653:       category,
08654:       intraoral,
08655:       intraoralDark,
08656:       confidence: category==='facial' ? (faceBox?.count?0.82:0.66) : ((redCount+whiteInside+darkCount)>80?0.82:0.62),
08657:       landmarks:{content:contentBox,face:faceBox||contentBox,mouth,teeth:teethFinal,midline:teethFinal.x+teethFinal.w*.5,incisal:teethFinal.y+teethFinal.h*.70,gingival:teethFinal.y+teethFinal.h*.18}
08658:     };
08659:   }
08660:   async function localAnalyze(dataUrl,forcedCategory){
08661:     const img=await loadImg(dataUrl);
08662:     let browserFace=null;
08663:     // Experimental browser-native detector. It gives only face boxes, not dental landmarks.
08664:     try{
08665:       if('FaceDetector' in window && !forcedCategory){
08666:         const fd=new FaceDetector({fastMode:true,maxDetectedFaces:1});
08667:         const faces=await fd.detect(img);
08668:         if(faces && faces[0]) browserFace=faces[0].boundingBox;
08669:       }
08670:     }catch(e){}
08671:     const result=analyzePixels(img,forcedCategory);
08672:     if(browserFace && !result.intraoral){
08673:       result.category='facial';
08674:       result.landmarks.face={x:browserFace.x/img.naturalWidth,y:browserFace.y/img.naturalHeight,w:browserFace.width/img.naturalWidth,h:browserFace.height/img.naturalHeight};
08675:       const f=result.landmarks.face;
08676:       result.landmarks.mouth={x:f.x+f.w*.28,y:f.y+f.h*.58,w:f.w*.44,h:f.h*.18};
08677:       result.confidence=.86;
08678:     }
08679:     return result;
08680:   }
08681:   window.caseflowLocalAnalyze = localAnalyze;
08682: 
08683:   // Upload-time confirmation popup: lets the clinician confirm/override how a Focus photo
08684:   // was taken (black contraster / ordinary retracted intraoral / not an intraoral close-up),
08685:   // since reflections or unusual backgrounds can fool the automatic detector. Resolves to
08686:   // { intraoral, intraoralDark, manual }. `auto` = { intraoral, intraoralDark } | null.
08687:   window.cfAskContraster = function(auto){
08688:     return new Promise(resolve=>{
08689:       const autoCat = (auto && auto.category) || '';
08690:       const autoKey = (auto && auto.intraoral)
08691:         ? (auto.intraoralDark ? 'dark' : 'retracted')
08692:         : (autoCat==='facial' ? 'facial' : 'focuslips');
08693:       // shot type => { intraoral, intraoralDark, lips }
08694:       const MAP = {
08695:         facial:    {intraoral:false, intraoralDark:false, lips:true },
08696:         focuslips: {intraoral:false, intraoralDark:false, lips:true },
08697:         retracted: {intraoral:true,  intraoralDark:false, lips:false},
08698:         dark:      {intraoral:true,  intraoralDark:true,  lips:false}
08699:       };
08700:       const labelOf = k => k==='dark' ? '黒コントラスター（リップなし）'
08701:         : k==='retracted' ? 'リトラクター（リップなし）'
08702:         : k==='facial' ? '顔貌・スマイル（リップあり）'
08703:         : '口腔内接写・リップあり';
08704:       if(!document.getElementById('cfCtStyle')){
08705:         const st=document.createElement('style'); st.id='cfCtStyle';
08706:         st.textContent='.cfCtOverlay{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:99999;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:rgba(16,12,30,.55);backdrop-filter:blur(3px);padding:22px}'
08707:           +'.cfCtCard{width:100%;max-width:360px;background:var(--cfUiPanelStrong,#fff);color:var(--cfUiText,#201b35);border-radius:18px;padding:18px 16px 14px;box-shadow:0 22px 60px rgba(16,12,30,.4)}'
08708:           +'.cfCtTitle{font-size:15px;font-weight:950;margin:0 2px 4px}'
08709:           +'.cfCtSub{font-size:11px;line-height:1.5;color:var(--cfUiSub,rgba(32,27,53,.6));margin:0 2px 14px}'
08710:           +'.cfCtBtn{display:block;width:100%;text-align:left;border:1.5px solid var(--cfUiBorder,rgba(32,27,53,.16));background:var(--cfUiPanel,rgba(255,255,255,.6));color:inherit;border-radius:13px;padding:11px 13px;margin-bottom:9px;font:inherit;font-size:13px;font-weight:850;cursor:pointer;transition:border-color .15s,background .15s}'
08711:           +'.cfCtBtn small{display:block;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:600;color:var(--cfUiSub,rgba(32,27,53,.55));margin-top:3px}'
08712:           +'.cfCtBtn:hover{border-color:var(--c1,#6a4df4)}'
08713:           +'.cfCtBtn.cfCtSel{border-color:var(--c1,#6a4df4);background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--c1,#6a4df4) 12%,transparent)}'
08714:           +'.cfCtBtn.cfCtSel::after{content:" ✓ 自動判定";font-size:10px;font-weight:900;color:var(--c1,#6a4df4)}'
08715:           +'.cfCtSkip{display:block;width:100%;text-align:center;background:none;border:none;color:var(--cfUiSub,rgba(32,27,53,.55));font:inherit;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;padding:8px 4px 2px;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline}';
08716:         document.head.appendChild(st);
08717:       }
08718:       const ov=document.createElement('div'); ov.className='cfCtOverlay';
08719:       ov.innerHTML='<div class="cfCtCard">'
08720:         +'<div class="cfCtTitle">この写真の撮影タイプは？</div>'
08721:         +'<div class="cfCtSub">リップが写っているか／コントラスターの有無で生成処理が変わります（リップなしの接写ではリップを生成しません）。'+(auto?('自動判定: <b>'+labelOf(autoKey)+'</b>'):'')+'</div>'
08722:         +'<button class="cfCtBtn" data-v="facial">顔貌・スマイル（リップあり）<small>顔全体／口元のスマイル写真。歯のみ補正し、顔・リップはそのまま</small></button>'
08723:         +'<button class="cfCtBtn" data-v="focuslips">口腔内接写・リップあり<small>リトラクターなしの接写でリップが写っている。リップはそのまま、歯のみ補正</small></button>'
08724:         +'<button class="cfCtBtn" data-v="retracted">リトラクター（リップなし）<small>頬粘膜を開けた接写・黒背景なし。リップは生成しない／歯肉・背景はそのまま</small></button>'
08725:         +'<button class="cfCtBtn" data-v="dark">黒コントラスター（リップなし）<small>黒背景・single arch の接写。背景を黒のまま、エナメルのみ補正</small></button>'
08726:         +'<button class="cfCtSkip" data-v="auto">自動判定にまかせる</button>'
08727:         +'</div>';
08728:       document.body.appendChild(ov);
08729:       const done=(v)=>{
08730:         ov.remove();
08731:         if(v==='auto'){
08732:           const ai=!!(auto&&auto.intraoral);
08733:           resolve({intraoral:ai, intraoralDark:!!(auto&&auto.intraoralDark), lips:!ai, manual:false});
08734:           return;
08735:         }
08736:         const m=MAP[v]||MAP.focuslips;
08737:         resolve({ intraoral:m.intraoral, intraoralDark:m.intraoralDark, lips:m.lips, manual:true });
08738:       };
08739:       ov.querySelectorAll('[data-v]').forEach(b=>{ if(b.dataset.v===autoKey) b.classList.add('cfCtSel'); b.addEventListener('click',()=>done(b.dataset.v)); });
08740:       ov.addEventListener('click',e=>{ if(e.target===ov) done('auto'); });
08741:     });
08742:   };

08744:   function overlayMarkup(p){
08745:     const lm=p.landmarks;if(!lm)return '';
08746:     const box=(b,cls)=>b?`<div class="box ${cls}" style="left:${b.x*100}%;top:${b.y*100}%;width:${b.w*100}%;height:${b.h*100}%"></div>`:'';
08747:     const lineV=lm.midline?`<div class="line v" style="left:${lm.midline*100}%"></div>`:'';
08748:     const lineH=lm.incisal?`<div class="line h" style="top:${lm.incisal*100}%"></div>`:'';
08749:     return `<div class="landmarkOverlay">${box(lm.mouth,'mouth')}${box(lm.teeth,'teeth')}${lineV}${lineH}</div>`;
08750:   }
08751:   function toolButtons(i,p){
08752:     return `<div class="tileActions"><button class="miniBtn" onclick="event.stopPropagation();runLocalDetect(${i})">Detect</button><button class="miniBtn subtle" onclick="event.stopPropagation();openCropModal(${i})">Crop</button><button class="miniBtn subtle" onclick="event.stopPropagation();removeFacialBg(${i})">BG</button></div>`;
08753:   }
08754:   window.renderUpload=function(){

08756:     for(let i=0;i<12;i++){

08760:         p.category=cat;
08761:         const intraoralBadge = p.intraoral ? `<span class="intraoralBadge">口腔内接写</span>` : '';
08762:         html += `<div class="tile categoryPriority"><img src="${p.dataUrl}">${overlayMarkup(p)}<span class="badge">${i+1}</span>${intraoralBadge}<div class="landmarkHint"><span>${p.intraoral?'Focus · 口腔内接写 (contraster)':(p.landmarks?'local landmarks':'facial / focus')}</span></div><button class="x" onclick="removePhoto(${i})">×</button><div class="ord"><button onclick="movePhoto(${i},-1)">‹</button><button onclick="movePhoto(${i},1)">›</button></div><input class="sel categoryText" value="${cat}" onchange="setCategoryText(${i},this.value)" onblur="setCategoryText(${i},this.value)" placeholder="facial / focus">${toolButtons(i,p)}</div>`;
08763:       }else html += `<button class="tile empty" onclick="$('fileInput').click()">${i+1}</button>`;
08764:     }
08765:     const grid=$id('uploadGrid'); if(grid) grid.innerHTML=html;
08766:   };
08767:   window.setCategoryText=function(i,v){

08774:   window.runLocalDetect=async function(i){

08779:     renderUpload();toast(`${res.category}${res.intraoral?' · 口腔内接写':''} / landmarks detected`);
08780:   };

08788:     }

08807:   function drawContainBox(ctx,img,box,alpha,zoom=1){
08808:     const cw=ctx.canvas.width,ch=ctx.canvas.height;
08809:     const sx=(box?.x||0)*img.naturalWidth, sy=(box?.y||0)*img.naturalHeight, sw=(box?.w||1)*img.naturalWidth, sh=(box?.h||1)*img.naturalHeight;
08810:     const s=Math.min(cw/sw,ch/sh)*zoom;
08811:     const w=sw*s,h=sh*s,x=(cw-w)/2,y=(ch-h)/2;
08812:     ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;ctx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';ctx.drawImage(img,sx,sy,sw,sh,x,y,w,h);ctx.restore();
08813:   }
08814:   function defaultBox(p){
08815:     const cat=parseCat(p?.category,'focus');
08816:     if(p?.landmarks?.mouth && cat==='focus') return expandBoxLocal(p.landmarks.mouth,.55,.70);
08817:     if(p?.landmarks?.face && cat==='facial') return expandBoxLocal(p.landmarks.face,.08,.10);
08818:     if(cat==='facial') return {x:.08,y:.02,w:.84,h:.96};
08819:     return {x:.04,y:.20,w:.92,h:.58};
08820:   }
08821:   function expandBoxLocal(b,px,py){
08822:     const x=clamp(b.x-b.w*px,0,1), y=clamp(b.y-b.h*py,0,1), r=clamp(b.x+b.w*(1+px),0,1), bot=clamp(b.y+b.h*(1+py),0,1);
08823:     return {x,y,w:r-x,h:bot-y};
08824:   }
08825:   const oldDrawMorph=window.drawMorph;
08826:   window.drawMorph=function(t=0){
08827:     const c=$id('morphCanvas'); if(!c){ if(oldDrawMorph)oldDrawMorph(t); return; }
08828:     if(window.sizeCanvas) sizeCanvas();
08829:     const ctx=c.getContext('2d');ctx.clearRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.fillRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
08830:     if(!state.photos.length||!state.imageCache?.length) return;
09354:     p.notes=$id('pmNotes').value.trim();
09355:     p.editState={rotate:ed.rotate||0,flipX:!!ed.flipX,flipY:!!ed.flipY,straighten:ed.straighten||0,zoom:ed.zoom||100,offsetX:ed.offsetX||0,offsetY:ed.offsetY||0};
09356:     const img = await loadImage(ed.baseDataUrl || p.dataUrl);
09357:     const out=document.createElement('canvas'); out.width=img.naturalWidth; out.height=img.naturalHeight;
09358:     const ctx=out.getContext('2d');
09359:     const keepAlpha = String(ed.baseDataUrl||p.dataUrl).startsWith('data:image/png');
09360:     if(!keepAlpha){ ctx.fillStyle='#fff'; ctx.fillRect(0,0,out.width,out.height); }
09361:     ctx.translate(out.width/2 + (ed.offsetX/100)*out.width*.26, out.height/2 + (ed.offsetY/100)*out.height*.26);
09362:     ctx.rotate(((ed.rotate||0)+(ed.straighten||0))*Math.PI/180);
09363:     ctx.scale(ed.flipX?-1:1, ed.flipY?-1:1);
09364:     const zoom=(ed.zoom||100)/100;
09365:     ctx.drawImage(img, -img.naturalWidth*zoom/2, -img.naturalHeight*zoom/2, img.naturalWidth*zoom, img.naturalHeight*zoom);
09366:     p.dataUrl = out.toDataURL(keepAlpha?'image/png':'image/jpeg', .95);
09367:     renderUpload(); if(window.renderPreview) renderPreview(); if(window.renderSimulator) renderSimulator(); closePhotoManager(); toastFn('Photo を更新しました');
09368:   }
