09425: <script>
09426: /* ---------- v5.5 manual animation / library / share / nav ---------- */
09427: (function(){
09428:   // `state` is a global lexical `const` (NOT window.state, which isn't assigned until a later
09429:   // script). Guarding on window.state made this whole block early-return, leaving
09430:   // saveCurrentProject/persistLibrary/etc. undefined → "Library に保存" silently failed.
09431:   if(typeof state==='undefined') return;
09432:   if(!window.state){ try{ window.state=state; }catch(e){} }
09433:   const $id=id=>document.getElementById(id);
09434:   const toastFn=msg=>window.toast?toast(msg):console.log(msg);
09435:   state.projectLibrary = state.projectLibrary || [];
09436:   state.manualAnim = state.manualAnim || {frames:[],selected:-1,playing:false,raf:null,playTimer:null,current:{base:-1,overlay:-1,opacity:55,scale:100,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,duration:70,label:''}};
09437: 
09438:   function todayStamp(){ return new Date().toLocaleString('ja-JP',{year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}); }
09439:   function loadLibrary(){ try{ state.projectLibrary = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('caseflow_project_library_v55')||'[]'); }catch(e){ state.projectLibrary=[]; } }

16352:   async function createSimulationImageV82(source){
16353:     const img=await loadImage(source.dataUrl);
16354:     const max=1400, scale=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.naturalWidth||img.width,img.naturalHeight||img.height));
16355:     const w=Math.round((img.naturalWidth||img.width)*scale), h=Math.round((img.naturalHeight||img.height)*scale);
16356:     const c=document.createElement('canvas'); c.width=w; c.height=h;
16357:     const ctx=c.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});
16358:     ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);
16359:     const s=state.simV82, t=s.targets||{}, amount=(s.amount||55)/100;
16360:     const x0=Math.round(w*.10), x1=Math.round(w*.90), bw=x1-x0;
16361:     const yc=Math.round(h*(clamp(s.band||50,25,75)/100));
16362:     const bh=Math.round(h*.30), y0=Math.max(0,Math.round(yc-bh/2)), y1=Math.min(h,Math.round(yc+bh/2)), bhh=y1-y0;
16363:     const shade=(t.shade?1:0)*amount*((s.shade||46)/55);
16364:     const squeeze=(t.space?1:0)*amount*.055;
16365:     const scallop=(t.scallop?1:0)*amount*((s.scallop||52)/55);
16366:     const incisal=(t.incisal?1:0)*amount;
16367:     const harmony=(t.harmony?1:0)*amount;
16368:     const missing=(t.missing?1:0)*amount*((s.missing||30)/55);
16369: 
16370:     if(squeeze>0 && bw>4 && bhh>4){
16371:       const tmp=document.createElement('canvas'); tmp.width=bw; tmp.height=bhh;
16372:       tmp.getContext('2d').drawImage(c,x0,y0,bw,bhh,0,0,bw,bhh);
16373:       const nw=bw*(1-squeeze); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha=.72; ctx.drawImage(tmp,0,0,bw,bhh,x0+(bw-nw)/2,y0,nw,bhh); ctx.restore();
16374:     }
16375:     if(shade>0 && bw>0 && bhh>0){
16376:       const id=ctx.getImageData(x0,y0,bw,bhh), d=id.data;
16377:       for(let i=0;i<d.length;i+=4){
16378:         const r=d[i],g=d[i+1],b=d[i+2]; const l=(r+g+b)/3, ch=Math.max(r,g,b)-Math.min(r,g,b);
16379:         if(l>92 && ch<125){
16380:           const k=shade*Math.min(1,(l-82)/160);
16381:           d[i]=Math.min(255,r+(255-r)*k*.58); d[i+1]=Math.min(255,g+(252-g)*k*.64); d[i+2]=Math.min(255,b+(246-b)*k*.74);
16382:         }
16383:       }
16384:       ctx.putImageData(id,x0,y0);
16385:     }
16386:     if(scallop>0){
16387:       ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha=Math.min(.38,.10+scallop*.30); ctx.fillStyle='#d99098';
16388:       const gy=y0+bhh*.10, N=8;
16389:       for(let i=1;i<N;i++){ const x=x0+bw*i/N; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x-6,gy); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, gy+10+scallop*4, x+6, gy); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); }
16390:       ctx.restore();
16391:     }
16198:     // Auto-pick from gallery only if the user has NOT explicitly dismissed this slot.
16199:     // Dismissal is set when 削除 is pressed; cleared when a new photo is explicitly uploaded.
16200:     const macro = state.simMacro || (state._simMacroExcluded ? null : pickCategoryPhoto('facial'));
16201:     const micro = state.simMicro || (state._simMicroExcluded ? null : pickCategoryPhoto('focus'));

16348:   function loadImage(src){ return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{ const img=new Image(); img.onload=()=>resolve(img); img.onerror=reject; img.src=src; }); }
16349:   function rr(ctx,x,y,w,h,r){ ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x+r,y); ctx.arcTo(x+w,y,x+w,y+h,r); ctx.arcTo(x+w,y+h,x,y+h,r); ctx.arcTo(x,y+h,x,y,r); ctx.arcTo(x,y,x+w,y,r); ctx.closePath(); }
16350:   function drawMark(ctx,w,h){ ctx.save(); const fs=Math.max(16,Math.round(w*.025)); ctx.font=`900 ${fs}px -apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,"Helvetica Neue",Arial,sans-serif`; ctx.textAlign='right'; ctx.textBaseline='bottom'; ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.92)'; ctx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.20)'; ctx.shadowBlur=10; ctx.fillText('CASEFLOW STUDIO™',w-Math.max(18,w*.035),h-Math.max(18,h*.035)); ctx.restore(); }

16352:   async function createSimulationImageV82(source){
16353:     const img=await loadImage(source.dataUrl);
16354:     const max=1400, scale=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.naturalWidth||img.width,img.naturalHeight||img.height));
16355:     const w=Math.round((img.naturalWidth||img.width)*scale), h=Math.round((img.naturalHeight||img.height)*scale);
16356:     const c=document.createElement('canvas'); c.width=w; c.height=h;
16357:     const ctx=c.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});
16358:     ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);
16359:     const s=state.simV82, t=s.targets||{}, amount=(s.amount||55)/100;
16360:     const x0=Math.round(w*.10), x1=Math.round(w*.90), bw=x1-x0;
16361:     const yc=Math.round(h*(clamp(s.band||50,25,75)/100));
16362:     const bh=Math.round(h*.30), y0=Math.max(0,Math.round(yc-bh/2)), y1=Math.min(h,Math.round(yc+bh/2)), bhh=y1-y0;
16363:     const shade=(t.shade?1:0)*amount*((s.shade||46)/55);
16364:     const squeeze=(t.space?1:0)*amount*.055;
16365:     const scallop=(t.scallop?1:0)*amount*((s.scallop||52)/55);
16366:     const incisal=(t.incisal?1:0)*amount;
16367:     const harmony=(t.harmony?1:0)*amount;
16368:     const missing=(t.missing?1:0)*amount*((s.missing||30)/55);
16369: 
16370:     if(squeeze>0 && bw>4 && bhh>4){
16371:       const tmp=document.createElement('canvas'); tmp.width=bw; tmp.height=bhh;
16372:       tmp.getContext('2d').drawImage(c,x0,y0,bw,bhh,0,0,bw,bhh);
16373:       const nw=bw*(1-squeeze); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha=.72; ctx.drawImage(tmp,0,0,bw,bhh,x0+(bw-nw)/2,y0,nw,bhh); ctx.restore();
16374:     }
16375:     if(shade>0 && bw>0 && bhh>0){
16376:       const id=ctx.getImageData(x0,y0,bw,bhh), d=id.data;
16377:       for(let i=0;i<d.length;i+=4){
16378:         const r=d[i],g=d[i+1],b=d[i+2]; const l=(r+g+b)/3, ch=Math.max(r,g,b)-Math.min(r,g,b);
16379:         if(l>92 && ch<125){
16380:           const k=shade*Math.min(1,(l-82)/160);
16381:           d[i]=Math.min(255,r+(255-r)*k*.58); d[i+1]=Math.min(255,g+(252-g)*k*.64); d[i+2]=Math.min(255,b+(246-b)*k*.74);
16382:         }
16383:       }
16384:       ctx.putImageData(id,x0,y0);
16385:     }
16386:     if(scallop>0){
16387:       ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha=Math.min(.38,.10+scallop*.30); ctx.fillStyle='#d99098';
16388:       const gy=y0+bhh*.10, N=8;
16389:       for(let i=1;i<N;i++){ const x=x0+bw*i/N; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x-6,gy); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, gy+10+scallop*4, x+6, gy); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); }
16390:       ctx.restore();
16391:     }
16392:     if(missing>0){
16241:       // highlights the auto-detected type.
16242:       if(micro && typeof micro.intraoral !== 'boolean' && window.caseflowLocalAnalyze){
16243:         window.caseflowLocalAnalyze(micro.dataUrl, micro.category||'focus')
16244:           .then(a=>{ micro.intraoral=!!(a&&a.intraoral); micro.intraoralDark=!!(a&&a.intraoralDark); if(typeof micro.lips!=='boolean') micro.lips=!micro.intraoral; updateSimulationChoicesV82(); })
16245:           .catch(()=>{ micro.intraoral=false; });
16246:       }
16247:     }
16248:     ensureSimCardActions(macroCard,'facial',macro);
16249:     ensureSimCardActions(microCard,'focus',micro);
16250:     // Remove legacy v6.6 "Not selected / Using selected image" badge that conflicts with
16251:     // the v8.2 "Facial image ready / Focus image ready" display.
16252:     [macroCard, microCard].forEach(card=>{
16253:       if(!card) return;
16254:       card.querySelector('.simChoiceState')?.remove();
16255:       // Also hide the original subtitle span that v6.6 overwrites with "未選択..." text,
16256:       // since v8.2 has .choiceFileMeta + .choiceState for the same info.
16257:       const inner=card.querySelector('div');
16258:       const origSpan=inner?.querySelector('span:not(.choiceFileMeta):not(.choiceState)');
16259:       if(origSpan) origSpan.style.display='none';
16260:     });
16261:     // Gate generate button: if a Micro image is loaded but shot type not yet confirmed, block.
16262:     const genBtn=document.querySelector('#simulator button.primary[onclick*="generateSimulation"]');
16263:     if(genBtn){
16264:       const unconfirmed=!!(micro&&!micro._shotConfirmed);
16265:       genBtn.disabled=unconfirmed;
16266:       genBtn.title=unconfirmed?'撮影タイプ（必須）を選択してください':'';
16267:     }

16348:   function loadImage(src){ return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{ const img=new Image(); img.onload=()=>resolve(img); img.onerror=reject; img.src=src; }); }
16349:   function rr(ctx,x,y,w,h,r){ ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x+r,y); ctx.arcTo(x+w,y,x+w,y+h,r); ctx.arcTo(x+w,y+h,x,y+h,r); ctx.arcTo(x,y+h,x,y,r); ctx.arcTo(x,y,x+w,y,r); ctx.closePath(); }
16350:   function drawMark(ctx,w,h){ ctx.save(); const fs=Math.max(16,Math.round(w*.025)); ctx.font=`900 ${fs}px -apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,"Helvetica Neue",Arial,sans-serif`; ctx.textAlign='right'; ctx.textBaseline='bottom'; ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.92)'; ctx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.20)'; ctx.shadowBlur=10; ctx.fillText('CASEFLOW STUDIO™',w-Math.max(18,w*.035),h-Math.max(18,h*.035)); ctx.restore(); }

16352:   async function createSimulationImageV82(source){
16353:     const img=await loadImage(source.dataUrl);
16354:     const max=1400, scale=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.naturalWidth||img.width,img.naturalHeight||img.height));
16355:     const w=Math.round((img.naturalWidth||img.width)*scale), h=Math.round((img.naturalHeight||img.height)*scale);
16356:     const c=document.createElement('canvas'); c.width=w; c.height=h;
16357:     const ctx=c.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});
16358:     ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);
16359:     const s=state.simV82, t=s.targets||{}, amount=(s.amount||55)/100;
16360:     const x0=Math.round(w*.10), x1=Math.round(w*.90), bw=x1-x0;
16361:     const yc=Math.round(h*(clamp(s.band||50,25,75)/100));
16362:     const bh=Math.round(h*.30), y0=Math.max(0,Math.round(yc-bh/2)), y1=Math.min(h,Math.round(yc+bh/2)), bhh=y1-y0;
16363:     const shade=(t.shade?1:0)*amount*((s.shade||46)/55);
16364:     const squeeze=(t.space?1:0)*amount*.055;
16365:     const scallop=(t.scallop?1:0)*amount*((s.scallop||52)/55);
16366:     const incisal=(t.incisal?1:0)*amount;
16367:     const harmony=(t.harmony?1:0)*amount;
16368:     const missing=(t.missing?1:0)*amount*((s.missing||30)/55);
16369: 
16370:     if(squeeze>0 && bw>4 && bhh>4){
16371:       const tmp=document.createElement('canvas'); tmp.width=bw; tmp.height=bhh;
16372:       tmp.getContext('2d').drawImage(c,x0,y0,bw,bhh,0,0,bw,bhh);
16373:       const nw=bw*(1-squeeze); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha=.72; ctx.drawImage(tmp,0,0,bw,bhh,x0+(bw-nw)/2,y0,nw,bhh); ctx.restore();
16374:     }
16375:     if(shade>0 && bw>0 && bhh>0){
16376:       const id=ctx.getImageData(x0,y0,bw,bhh), d=id.data;
16377:       for(let i=0;i<d.length;i+=4){
16378:         const r=d[i],g=d[i+1],b=d[i+2]; const l=(r+g+b)/3, ch=Math.max(r,g,b)-Math.min(r,g,b);
16379:         if(l>92 && ch<125){
16380:           const k=shade*Math.min(1,(l-82)/160);
16381:           d[i]=Math.min(255,r+(255-r)*k*.58); d[i+1]=Math.min(255,g+(252-g)*k*.64); d[i+2]=Math.min(255,b+(246-b)*k*.74);
16382:         }
16383:       }
16384:       ctx.putImageData(id,x0,y0);
16385:     }
16386:     if(scallop>0){
16387:       ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha=Math.min(.38,.10+scallop*.30); ctx.fillStyle='#d99098';
16388:       const gy=y0+bhh*.10, N=8;
16389:       for(let i=1;i<N;i++){ const x=x0+bw*i/N; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x-6,gy); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, gy+10+scallop*4, x+6, gy); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); }
16390:       ctx.restore();
16391:     }
16392:     if(missing>0){
16393:       ctx.save();
16394:       ctx.globalAlpha=Math.min(.42,.10+missing*.28);
16395:       ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.86)';
16396:       const rx=w*.60, ry=yc-h*.015, rw=w*.085, rh=h*.12;
16397:       rr(ctx,rx,ry,rw,rh,Math.max(12,w*.01)); ctx.fill();
16398:       ctx.globalAlpha=.18+missing*.16;
16399:       ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(32,27,53,.22)'; ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2,w*.0032); rr(ctx,rx,ry,rw,rh,Math.max(12,w*.01)); ctx.stroke();
16400:       ctx.restore();
16401:     }
16402:     if(harmony>0){
16403:       ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha=.10+harmony*.16; ctx.fillStyle='#fff'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(w*.5,yc,w*.33,h*.045,0,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
16404:       ctx.globalAlpha=.12+harmony*.14; ctx.strokeStyle='#fff'; ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2,w*.0035); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(w*.26,yc+h*.042); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(w*.5,yc+h*.076,w*.74,yc+h*.042); ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore();
16405:     }
16406:     if(incisal>0){
16407:       ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha=.20+incisal*.22; ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.92)'; ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2,w*.004); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(w*.30,yc+h*.055); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(w*.5,yc+h*.082,w*.70,yc+h*.055); ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore();
16408:     }
16409:     ctx.save(); ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.84)'; rr(ctx,w*.035,h*.035,w*.50,Math.max(44,h*.062),22); ctx.fill();
16410:     ctx.fillStyle='#201b35'; ctx.font=`900 ${Math.max(16,Math.round(w/38))}px -apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,sans-serif`; ctx.fillText('Simulation / Reference',w*.055,h*.075);
16411:     ctx.fillStyle='rgba(32,27,53,.55)'; ctx.font=`760 ${Math.max(10,Math.round(w/80))}px -apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,sans-serif`; ctx.fillText('For explanation only',w*.055,h*.103); ctx.restore();
16412:     drawMark(ctx,w,h);
16413:     return c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',.93);
16414:   }

16416:   window.generateSimulation = async function(){
16417:     const source=state.simMicro || state.simMacro || pickCategoryPhoto('focus') || pickCategoryPhoto('facial') || (state.photos||[])[0];
16418:     if(!source){ toastFn('Simulation Studio で使用する画像をアップロードしてください'); return; }
16419:     state._simSource = source;
16420:     toastFn('Reference Image を作成中…');
16421:     try{
16422:       const result=await createSimulationImageV82(source);
16423:       state.simResult = { dataUrl:result, source:source.dataUrl, sourceKind:(source.category==='facial'?'Facial':source.category==='focus'?'Focus':'Image'), createdAt:new Date().toISOString(), settings:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.simV82||{})) };
16424:       if(typeof go==='function') go('result');
16425:       if(typeof renderResult==='function') setTimeout(()=>renderResult(),80);
16426:     }catch(err){ console.error(err); toastFn('画像の作成に失敗しました'); }
16427:   };

16429:   const prevRenderSimulator=window.renderSimulator;
16430:   window.renderSimulator=function(){
16431:     if(prevRenderSimulator) prevRenderSimulator();
16432:     const p=$id('simFinishPanel'); if(p) renderSimFinishPanelV82();
16433:     bindSimulationUploadsV82();
16434:     const primary=document.querySelector('#simulator button.primary[onclick*="generateSimulation"]');
16435:     if(primary) primary.innerHTML='Reference Image 作成';
16253:       if(!card) return;
16254:       card.querySelector('.simChoiceState')?.remove();
16255:       // Also hide the original subtitle span that v6.6 overwrites with "未選択..." text,
16256:       // since v8.2 has .choiceFileMeta + .choiceState for the same info.
16257:       const inner=card.querySelector('div');
16258:       const origSpan=inner?.querySelector('span:not(.choiceFileMeta):not(.choiceState)');
16259:       if(origSpan) origSpan.style.display='none';
16260:     });
16261:     // Gate generate button: if a Micro image is loaded but shot type not yet confirmed, block.
16262:     const genBtn=document.querySelector('#simulator button.primary[onclick*="generateSimulation"]');
16263:     if(genBtn){
16264:       const unconfirmed=!!(micro&&!micro._shotConfirmed);
16265:       genBtn.disabled=unconfirmed;
16266:       genBtn.title=unconfirmed?'撮影タイプ（必須）を選択してください':'';
16267:     }
16268:   }

16270:   async function resizeAdvancedLocal(file){
16271:     if(typeof resizeAdvanced==='function') return await resizeAdvanced(file);
16272:     if(typeof resize==='function') return await resize(file);
16273:     return await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const fr=new FileReader(); fr.onload=()=>resolve(fr.result); fr.onerror=reject; fr.readAsDataURL(file);});
16274:   }

16276:   function bindSimulationUploadsV82(){
16277:     const oldMacro=$id('simMacroInput'), oldMicro=$id('simMicroInput');
16278:     if(!oldMacro||!oldMicro) return;
16279:     const macroInput=oldMacro.cloneNode(true), microInput=oldMicro.cloneNode(true);
16280:     oldMacro.parentNode.replaceChild(macroInput, oldMacro);
16281:     oldMicro.parentNode.replaceChild(microInput, oldMicro);
16282:     macroInput.addEventListener('change', async e=>{
16283:       const f=e.target.files && e.target.files[0]; if(!f) return;
16284:       const dataUrl=await resizeAdvancedLocal(f);
16285:       let intra=false, dark=false, lm=null, aMac=null;
16286:       try{ if(window.caseflowLocalAnalyze){ aMac=await window.caseflowLocalAnalyze(dataUrl,'facial'); intra=!!aMac.intraoral; dark=!!aMac.intraoralDark; lm=aMac.landmarks; } }catch(_){}
16287:       // Facial portraits inherently have lips — no shot-type picker needed on this card.
16288:       state._simMacroExcluded=false;
16289:       state.simMacro={dataUrl, label:'Macro / Facial', category:'facial', intraoral:intra, intraoralDark:dark, lips:!intra, _shotConfirmed:true, macroHasBrackets:false, landmarks:lm, name:f.name||'', date:new Date().toISOString()};
16290:       updateSimulationChoicesV82();
16291:       toastFn('Facial image を設定しました');
16292:       e.target.value='';
16293:     });
16294:     microInput.addEventListener('change', async e=>{
16295:       const f=e.target.files && e.target.files[0]; if(!f) return;
16296:       const dataUrl=await resizeAdvancedLocal(f);
16297:       let a=null;
16298:       try{ if(window.caseflowLocalAnalyze) a=await window.caseflowLocalAnalyze(dataUrl,'focus'); }catch(_){}
16299:       // No popup at upload: seed flags from auto-detection; the shot-type picker inside the
16300:       // Micro/Focus card is where the user confirms / corrects リップの有無・コントラスター.
16301:       const intra=!!(a&&a.intraoral), dark=!!(a&&a.intraoralDark);
16302:       state._simMicroExcluded=false;
16303:       state.simMicro={dataUrl, label:'Micro / Focus', category:'focus', intraoral:intra, intraoralDark:dark, lips:!intra, landmarks:a?a.landmarks:null, name:f.name||'', date:new Date().toISOString()};
16304:       updateSimulationChoicesV82();
16305:       toastFn('Focus image を設定しました');
16306:       e.target.value='';
16307:     });
16308:     const macroCard=$id('macroChoice'), microCard=$id('microChoice');
16309:     if(macroCard) macroCard.onclick=()=>macroInput.click();
16310:     if(microCard) microCard.onclick=()=>microInput.click();
16311:     updateSimulationChoicesV82();
16312:   }

20596:   function renderResultImages(){
20597:     // Seed the gallery from a legacy single simResult (e.g. loaded project).
20598:     if((!Array.isArray(state.simResults)||!state.simResults.length) && state.simResult && state.simResult.dataUrl){
20599:       state.simResults=[state.simResult]; state.simResultIndex=0;
20600:     }
20601:     const arr=Array.isArray(state.simResults)?state.simResults:[];
20602:     const r=state.simResult || arr[state.simResultIndex||0], src=state._simSource||pickSource();
20603:     if(!r||!src){ renderSimGallery(); return; }
20604:     const before=$id('resultBefore'), after=$id('resultAfter');
20605:     // ⑤ ALWAYS pair the before image with THIS result's OWN source (never the global
20606:     // _simSource, which is stale when switching between variations generated from different
20607:     // uploads). r.source / r.srcObj are stored per-result in pushSimResult/regenerate.
20608:     const beforeSrc=(r.source) || (r.srcObj && r.srcObj.dataUrl) || src.dataUrl;
20609:     if(before) before.src=beforeSrc;
20610:     if(after)  after.src=r.dataUrl;
20611:     const cap=$id('resultCaption');
20612:     if(cap) cap.textContent=`${sourceKindLabel(src)} image / Reference Image — 説明用の参考イメージであり、治療結果の保証ではありません。`;
20613:     if(typeof setupCompareSlider==='function'){ const el=$id('simulationCompareSlider'); if(el) setupCompareSlider(el); }
20614:     renderSimGallery();
20615:   }

20620:     let tag=$id('simV96Busy');

20626:   // Guarantee the contraster intraoral flag is resolved before we generate, regardless of
20627:   // which upload path created the source object. Mutates the source in place (and thus the
20628:   // shared _simSource reference) so the enamel mask + caption pick it up.
20629:   /* CF_PRIMARY_HARD_DEADLINE_V3 */
20630:   async function ensureIntraoralFlag(src){
20631:     if(!src || !src.dataUrl) return false;
20632:     const resolveLips=()=>{
20633:       if(typeof src.lips!=='boolean') src.lips=(((src.category||'').toLowerCase()==='facial') && !src.intraoral);
20634:     };
20635:     if(typeof src.intraoral==='boolean' && typeof src.intraoralDark==='boolean'){ resolveLips(); return src.intraoral; }
20636:     try{
20637:       if(window.caseflowLocalAnalyze){
20638:         const timeoutToken={cfTimeout:true};
20639:         const a=await Promise.race([
20640:           window.caseflowLocalAnalyze(src.dataUrl,src.category||null),
20641:           new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(()=>resolve(timeoutToken),650))
20642:         ]);
20643:         if(a!==timeoutToken){
20644:           src.intraoral=!!(a&&a.intraoral); src.intraoralDark=!!(a&&a.intraoralDark);
20645:           resolveLips(); return src.intraoral;
20646:         }
20647:       }
20648:     }catch(e){}
20649:     // Generation may never wait for analysis. Category and upload controls are the safe default.
20650:     if(typeof src.intraoral!=='boolean') src.intraoral=((src.category||'').toLowerCase()==='intraoral');
20651:     if(typeof src.intraoralDark!=='boolean') src.intraoralDark=false;
20652:     resolveLips(); return src.intraoral;
20653:   }
20654:   window.caseflowEnsureIntraoral=ensureIntraoralFlag;
20655:   // Produce the after-image: photoreal AI backend when enabled+configured, else the local
20656:   // artifact-free colour/finish pass.
20657:   const CF_PRIMARY_RESULT_DEADLINE_MS=18000;
20658:   const CF_PRIMARY_PREFLIGHT_MS=700;
20659:   async function cfFastPrimaryPreview(src,raw){
20660:     if(!src||!src.dataUrl) return null;
20661:     try{
20662:       const img=await Promise.race([
20663:         loadImage(src.dataUrl),
20664:         new Promise((_,reject)=>setTimeout(()=>reject(new Error('quick preview decode timeout')),1800))
20665:       ]);
20666:       const iw=img.naturalWidth||img.width,ih=img.naturalHeight||img.height;
20667:       if(!iw||!ih) return src.dataUrl;
20668:       const maxEdge=960,scale=Math.min(1,maxEdge/Math.max(iw,ih));
20669:       const w=Math.max(1,Math.round(iw*scale)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(ih*scale));
20670:       const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=w;c.height=h;
20671:       const x=c.getContext('2d',{alpha:false});x.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;x.imageSmoothingQuality='high';x.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);
20672:       // A deliberately conservative, GPU-light optical preview. It never claims structural AI accuracy.
20673:       const facial=((src.category||'').toLowerCase()==='facial')||(!src.intraoral&&src.lips!==false);
20674:       const rx=facial?w*.31:w*.12, ry=facial?h*.50:h*.18, rw=facial?w*.38:w*.76, rh=facial?h*.23:h*.55;
20675:       const tmp=document.createElement('canvas');tmp.width=w;tmp.height=h;
20676:       const tx=tmp.getContext('2d');
20677:       const shade=((raw&&raw.shade)!=null?raw.shade:50); const bright=1+Math.max(0,shade-50)/50*.07;
20678:       tx.filter='brightness('+bright.toFixed(3)+') saturate(.92) contrast(1.025)';tx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);tx.filter='none';
20679:       x.save();x.beginPath();x.ellipse(rx+rw/2,ry+rh/2,rw/2,rh/2,0,0,Math.PI*2);x.clip();x.globalAlpha=.74;x.drawImage(tmp,0,0);x.restore();
20680:       try{drawBadge(x,w,h);}catch(e){}
20681:       const out=c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',.82);c.width=c.height=1;tmp.width=tmp.height=1;return out;
20682:     }catch(e){ console.warn('[CaseFlow] quick preview fallback uses source',e); return src.dataUrl; }
20683:   }
20684:   window.cfFastPrimaryPreview=cfFastPrimaryPreview;
20685:   async function produceImage(src){
20686:     const started=performance.now();
20687:     let progressTimer=null, deadlineTimer=null;
20688:     window.__caseflowQuickPreviewUsed=false;
20689:     try{sessionStorage.setItem('cf_primary_generation_guard_v3',JSON.stringify({startedAt:Date.now()}));}catch(e){}
20690:     try{if(window.cfWait)cfWait.show('AIシミュレーション中',0);}catch(e){}
20691:     const quickTask=cfFastPrimaryPreview(src,state.simV96||{});
20692:     try{
20693:       await Promise.allSettled([
20694:         Promise.race([ensureIntraoralFlag(src),new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,CF_PRIMARY_PREFLIGHT_MS))]),
20695:         Promise.race([ensureProblemList(src),new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,CF_PRIMARY_PREFLIGHT_MS))])
20696:       ]);
20697:     }catch(e){}
20698:     const preflightMs=Math.round(performance.now()-started);
20699:     const aiOn=aiEnabled();
20700:     if(aiOn){
20701:       const msgs=['画像を最適化中…','AIシミュレーション生成中…','臨床デザインを確認中…'];let mi=0;
20702:       const tick=()=>{try{const q=document.querySelector('.cfWaitOverlay .cfWaitSub');if(q)q.textContent=msgs[mi++%msgs.length];}catch(e){}};
20703:       tick();progressTimer=setInterval(tick,2200);
20704:     }
20705:     try{
20706:       if(aiOn){
20707:         const aiTask=generateViaAI(src).then(url=>({kind:'ai',url})).catch(err=>({kind:'error',err}));
20708:         const remaining=Math.max(2500,CF_PRIMARY_RESULT_DEADLINE_MS-(performance.now()-started));
20709:         const deadlineTask=new Promise(resolve=>{deadlineTimer=setTimeout(()=>resolve({kind:'deadline'}),remaining);});
20710:         const result=await Promise.race([aiTask,deadlineTask]);
20711:         if(result&&result.kind==='ai'&&result.url){
20712:           window.__caseflowPrimaryResultMode='ai';return result.url;
20713:         }
20714:         if(result&&result.kind==='error'&&result.err&&result.err.cfNoTokens) throw result.err;
20715:         if(result&&result.kind==='deadline'){try{if(window.__cfGenerationAbort)window.__cfGenerationAbort();}catch(e){}}
20716:         if(result&&result.kind==='error') window.__caseflowLastAIError=String((result.err&&result.err.message)||result.err||'AI error');
20717:       }
20718:       // Absolute contract: always resolve the first result screen without invoking the heavy
20719:       // full-frame local pixel/MediaPipe fallback after an AI stall.
20720:       let quick=null;
20721:       try{quick=await Promise.race([quickTask,new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(()=>resolve(src&&src.dataUrl||null),1200))]);}catch(e){quick=src&&src.dataUrl||null;}
20722:       if(!quick) throw new Error('一次プレビューを作成できませんでした');
20723:       window.__caseflowQuickPreviewUsed=true;window.__caseflowPrimaryResultMode='quick';
20724:       setTimeout(()=>{try{toastFn('通信が時間内に完了しなかったため、軽量プレビューを表示しました。精密生成は結果画面から再実行できます。');}catch(e){}},80);
20725:       return quick;
20726:     } finally {
20727:       clearInterval(progressTimer);clearTimeout(deadlineTimer);
20728:       const totalMs=Math.round(performance.now()-started);
20729:       window.__caseflowGenerationTiming={preflight_ms:preflightMs,total_ms:totalMs,mode:window.__caseflowPrimaryResultMode||'unknown',hard_deadline_ms:CF_PRIMARY_RESULT_DEADLINE_MS,completed_at:new Date().toISOString()};
20730:       try{if(typeof window.cfLogEvent==='function')window.cfLogEvent('generation_timing','visual-simulation',window.__caseflowGenerationTiming);}catch(e){}
20731:       try{sessionStorage.removeItem('cf_primary_generation_guard_v3');}catch(e){}
20732:       try{if(window.cfWait)cfWait.hideAll();}catch(e){}
20733:     }
20734:   }
20735:   window.caseflowProduceSimImage=produceImage;

20737:   async function regenerate(){
20738:     if(regenBusy){ regenQueued=true; return; }
20739:     const src=state._simSource||pickSource();
20740:     if(!src) return;
20741:     regenBusy=true; setBusy(true);
20742:     try{
20743:       const url=await produceImage(src);
20744:       // Slider edits UPDATE the currently selected gallery slot in place (other variations
20745:       // stay intact), so re-editing never makes a previously generated image disappear.
20746:       const r=Object.assign({}, state.simResult, {
20747:         dataUrl:url, source:src.dataUrl, srcObj:src, sourceKind:sourceKind(src),
20748:         createdAt:new Date().toISOString(), settings:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.simV96))
20749:       });
20750:       if(Array.isArray(state.simResults) && state.simResults.length){ state.simResults[state.simResultIndex||0]=r; }
20751:       else { state.simResults=[r]; state.simResultIndex=0; }
20752:       state.simResult=r;
20753:       cfAutoSaveTempSim(r);   // ① keep the 48h temp Library copy in sync with slider edits
20754:       renderResultImages();
20755:       // Persist the new result immediately so a reload won't lose it
20756:       try{ if(window.cfPersist) cfPersist.schedule(1000); }catch(e){}
20757:     }catch(e){ console.error(e); toastFn('再生成に失敗しました'); }
20758:     regenBusy=false; setBusy(false);
20759:     if(regenQueued){ regenQueued=false; regenerate(); }
20760:   }
20761:   function scheduleRegen(delay){
20762:     clearTimeout(regenTimer);
20763:     regenTimer=setTimeout(regenerate, delay==null?60:delay);
20764:   }

20786:     const intentRow=`<div class="simV96AiRow" id="cfIntentRow">

20690:     try{if(window.cfWait)cfWait.show('AIシミュレーション中',0);}catch(e){}

20694:         Promise.race([ensureIntraoralFlag(src),new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,CF_PRIMARY_PREFLIGHT_MS))]),
20695:         Promise.race([ensureProblemList(src),new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,CF_PRIMARY_PREFLIGHT_MS))])
20696:       ]);
20697:     }catch(e){}
20698:     const preflightMs=Math.round(performance.now()-started);
20699:     const aiOn=aiEnabled();
20700:     if(aiOn){

20703:       tick();progressTimer=setInterval(tick,2200);
20704:     }
20705:     try{
20706:       if(aiOn){
20707:         const aiTask=generateViaAI(src).then(url=>({kind:'ai',url})).catch(err=>({kind:'error',err}));
20708:         const remaining=Math.max(2500,CF_PRIMARY_RESULT_DEADLINE_MS-(performance.now()-started));
20709:         const deadlineTask=new Promise(resolve=>{deadlineTimer=setTimeout(()=>resolve({kind:'deadline'}),remaining);});
20710:         const result=await Promise.race([aiTask,deadlineTask]);
20711:         if(result&&result.kind==='ai'&&result.url){
20712:           window.__caseflowPrimaryResultMode='ai';return result.url;
20713:         }
20714:         if(result&&result.kind==='error'&&result.err&&result.err.cfNoTokens) throw result.err;
20715:         if(result&&result.kind==='deadline'){try{if(window.__cfGenerationAbort)window.__cfGenerationAbort();}catch(e){}}
20716:         if(result&&result.kind==='error') window.__caseflowLastAIError=String((result.err&&result.err.message)||result.err||'AI error');
20717:       }
20718:       // Absolute contract: always resolve the first result screen without invoking the heavy
20719:       // full-frame local pixel/MediaPipe fallback after an AI stall.
20720:       let quick=null;
20721:       try{quick=await Promise.race([quickTask,new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(()=>resolve(src&&src.dataUrl||null),1200))]);}catch(e){quick=src&&src.dataUrl||null;}
20722:       if(!quick) throw new Error('一次プレビューを作成できませんでした');
20723:       window.__caseflowQuickPreviewUsed=true;window.__caseflowPrimaryResultMode='quick';
20724:       setTimeout(()=>{try{toastFn('通信が時間内に完了しなかったため、軽量プレビューを表示しました。精密生成は結果画面から再実行できます。');}catch(e){}},80);
20725:       return quick;
20726:     } finally {
20727:       clearInterval(progressTimer);clearTimeout(deadlineTimer);
20728:       const totalMs=Math.round(performance.now()-started);
20729:       window.__caseflowGenerationTiming={preflight_ms:preflightMs,total_ms:totalMs,mode:window.__caseflowPrimaryResultMode||'unknown',hard_deadline_ms:CF_PRIMARY_RESULT_DEADLINE_MS,completed_at:new Date().toISOString()};
20730:       try{if(typeof window.cfLogEvent==='function')window.cfLogEvent('generation_timing','visual-simulation',window.__caseflowGenerationTiming);}catch(e){}
20731:       try{sessionStorage.removeItem('cf_primary_generation_guard_v3');}catch(e){}
20732:       try{if(window.cfWait)cfWait.hideAll();}catch(e){}
20733:     }
20734:   }
20735:   window.caseflowProduceSimImage=produceImage;
20736: 
20737:   async function regenerate(){
20738:     if(regenBusy){ regenQueued=true; return; }
20739:     const src=state._simSource||pickSource();
20740:     if(!src) return;
20741:     regenBusy=true; setBusy(true);
20742:     try{
20743:       const url=await produceImage(src);
20744:       // Slider edits UPDATE the currently selected gallery slot in place (other variations
20745:       // stay intact), so re-editing never makes a previously generated image disappear.
20746:       const r=Object.assign({}, state.simResult, {
20747:         dataUrl:url, source:src.dataUrl, srcObj:src, sourceKind:sourceKind(src),
20748:         createdAt:new Date().toISOString(), settings:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.simV96))
20749:       });
20750:       if(Array.isArray(state.simResults) && state.simResults.length){ state.simResults[state.simResultIndex||0]=r; }
20751:       else { state.simResults=[r]; state.simResultIndex=0; }
20752:       state.simResult=r;
20753:       cfAutoSaveTempSim(r);   // ① keep the 48h temp Library copy in sync with slider edits
20754:       renderResultImages();
20755:       // Persist the new result immediately so a reload won't lose it
20756:       try{ if(window.cfPersist) cfPersist.schedule(1000); }catch(e){}
20757:     }catch(e){ console.error(e); toastFn('再生成に失敗しました'); }
20758:     regenBusy=false; setBusy(false);
20759:     if(regenQueued){ regenQueued=false; regenerate(); }
20760:   }
20761:   function scheduleRegen(delay){
20762:     clearTimeout(regenTimer);
20763:     regenTimer=setTimeout(regenerate, delay==null?60:delay);
20764:   }

20766:   function buildEditGrid(){
20767:     const grid=$id('simV96EditGrid');
20768:     if(!grid) return;
20769:     state.simV96=Object.assign({}, DEF, state.simV96||{});
20770:     const legend=`<div class="simV96Legend"><span>現状 / none</span><span>EBM</span><span>最大 / max</span></div>`+
20771:       `<div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:var(--cfUiSub,rgba(32,27,53,.6));margin:2px 0 8px;text-align:right">シミュレーション作成：2 tokens ／ 再生成：2 tokens</div>`;
20772:     const cpVal=String(state.simCustomPrompt||'');
20773:     const cpEsc=cpVal.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;');
20774:     // NOTE: the "AI backend" status line and endpoint-URL input were intentionally removed
20775:     // from the UI — system/connection details must never be visible on a chairside screen
20776:     // shown to patients. The backend connection itself is unchanged: aiEndpoint() resolves a
20777:     // built-in default endpoint, so generation works silently without any visible control.
20778:     const aiRow=`<div class="simV96AiRow">
20779:         <div class="simV96AiHead"><b>追加指示 / 主訴メモ</b><span id="simV96CpCount">${cpVal.length}/500</span></div>
20780:         <textarea id="simV96CustomPrompt" maxlength="500" rows="3" placeholder="例：正中離開だけ閉じたい。矮小歯は残す。歯列は少し野暮ったさを残す。右上1番の変色を改善。（500字以内・自由記述）" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;border-radius:12px;border:1px solid var(--cfUiBorder,rgba(32,27,53,.16));background:var(--cfUiPanelStrong,rgba(255,255,255,.9));color:var(--cfUiText,#201b35);font:inherit;font-size:12px;padding:10px;resize:vertical;line-height:1.5">${cpEsc}</textarea>
20781:       </div>`;
20782:     // v9.7 intent controls — 反映モード / 最小変更 / 補正範囲
20783:     state.simIntentMode=state.simIntentMode||'balanced';
20784:     state.simCorrectionScope=state.simCorrectionScope||'combined';
20785:     const imBtn=(v,label)=>`<button type="button" data-cfim="${v}" style="flex:1;padding:8px 4px;border-radius:10px;font:inherit;font-size:11px;font-weight:850;cursor:pointer;border:${state.simIntentMode===v?'0':'1.5px solid var(--cfUiBorder,rgba(32,27,53,.16))'};background:${state.simIntentMode===v?'linear-gradient(135deg,#6a3de8,#7c5cfc)':'var(--cfUiPanelStrong,rgba(255,255,255,.9))'};color:${state.simIntentMode===v?'#fff':'var(--cfUiText,#201b35)'}">${label}</button>`;
20786:     const intentRow=`<div class="simV96AiRow" id="cfIntentRow">
20787:         <div class="simV96AiHead"><b>意図の反映 / User Intent</b></div>
20788:         <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin-bottom:8px" id="cfIntentModeBtns">${imBtn('auto','Auto<br><span style="font-weight:700;font-size:9.5px">AI所見を優先</span>')}${imBtn('balanced','Balanced<br><span style="font-weight:700;font-size:9.5px">両立（推奨）</span>')}${imBtn('user-first','User-first<br><span style="font-weight:700;font-size:9.5px">指示を最優先</span>')}</div>
20789:         <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap">
